724:     private void updatePrintSequenceUi() {
725:         if (printSequenceButton == null || printSequenceSummary == null) return;
726:         if (printSequence == null) printSequence = new PrintSequence();
727:         printSequenceButton.setText("PIANO DI STAMPA");
728: 
729:         String base = recipeBaseSummary();
730:         boolean hasRecipe = !base.isEmpty() || !printSequence.isEmpty()
731:                 || (exposureRecipe != null && exposureRecipe.globalQuarterStops != 0);
732:         if (!hasRecipe) {
733:             printSequenceSummary.setText("");
734:             printSequenceSummary.setVisibility(View.GONE);
735:             return;
736:         }
737: 
738:         StringBuilder s = new StringBuilder();
739:         if (!base.isEmpty()) s.append(base);
740:         if (s.length() > 0) s.append("\n\n");
741:         s.append("ESPOSIZIONE\n");
742:         if (printSequence.hasSplit()) {
743:             s.append(printSequence.split.softLine()).append('\n').append(printSequence.split.hardLine());
744:         } else {
745:             s.append("SINGOLA · ").append(formatTime(printWidthMs));
746:             if (exposureRecipe != null && exposureRecipe.hasBase()) {
747:                 String f = exposureRecipe.filterLabel();
748:                 if (!"NESSUNO".equals(f)) s.append(" · ").append(f);
749:                 s.append(" · ").append(exposureRecipe.densityLabel());
750:             }
751:         }
752: 
753:         if (!printSequence.corrections.isEmpty()) {
754:             s.append("\n\nCORREZIONI");
755:             for (PrintCorrection c : printSequence.corrections) {
756:                 if (c == null) continue;
757:                 int baseMs = printSequence.baseMsFor(c, printWidthMs);
758:                 s.append('\n').append(c.displayLine(baseMs, printSequence.hasSplit()));
759:             }
760:         }
761:         if (exposureRecipe != null && exposureRecipe.globalQuarterStops != 0)
762:             s.append("\n\nCORREZIONE GLOBALE · ").append(exposureRecipe.globalLabel());
763:         printSequenceSummary.setText(s.toString());
764:         printSequenceSummary.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
765:     }

767:     private void persistPrintSequence() {
768:         if (printSequence == null) printSequence = new PrintSequence();
769:         getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putString("printSequence", printSequence.encode()).apply();
770:         updatePrintSequenceUi();
771:         applyModeUi();
772:     }

855:     private void showSplitGradeEditor(final boolean creating) {
856:         if (darkroomMode) return;
857:         if (printSequence == null) printSequence = new PrintSequence();
858:         final SplitGradePlan original = printSequence.split == null ? new SplitGradePlan() : printSequence.split.copy();
859:         final SplitGradePlan draft = original.copy();
860:         draft.enabled = true;
861:         if (creating) {
862:             if (printWidthMs < 1000) {
863:                 Toast.makeText(this, "Per lo Split Grade la base deve essere almeno 1,0 s", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
864:                 return;
865:             }
866:             // Default operativo di riferimento: Fomaspeed Variant 311 RC + testa colore Meopta.
867:             draft.softYellow = 60;
868:             draft.hardMagenta = 180;
934:         htMinus.setOnClickListener(v -> { hmsec[0]=Math.max(500,hmsec[0]-500); htValue.setText(formatTime(hmsec[0])); });
935:         htPlus.setOnClickListener(v -> { hmsec[0]=Math.min(36000000,hmsec[0]+500); htValue.setText(formatTime(hmsec[0])); });
936: 
937:         TextView limit = text("Somma massima delle due esposizioni: " + formatTime(printWidthMs) + " · base operativa", 11, MUTED, true);
938:         limit.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER); panel.addView(limit, margin(lp(-1,-2),0,2,0,5));
939:         TextView voice = text("Guida vocale: “Imposta Giallo " + sy[0] + "…” poi “Imposta Magenta " + hm[0] + "…”. Funziona anche a display spento.", 11, MUTED, false);
940:         voice.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER); panel.addView(voice, margin(lp(-1,-2),0,4,0,10));
941: 
942:         Button save = compactButton("SALVA SPLIT GRADE");
943:         save.setTextColor(Color.WHITE); save.setBackground(roundRect(SPLIT_VIVA_MAGENTA,9,0,0));
944:         save.setOnClickListener(v -> {
945:             if (sm[0] + hmsec[0] > printWidthMs) {
946:                 Toast.makeText(this, "La somma dello Split Grade non può superare la base " + formatTime(printWidthMs), Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
947:                 return;
948:             }
949:             draft.enabled=true; draft.softYellow=sy[0]; draft.softMs=sm[0]; draft.hardMagenta=hm[0]; draft.hardMs=hmsec[0]; draft.sanitize();
950:             printSequence.split=draft;
951:             for (PrintCorrection c : printSequence.corrections) if (PrintCorrection.PHASE_BASE.equals(c.phase)) c.phase=PrintCorrection.PHASE_SOFT;
952:             persistPrintSequence(); dialog.dismiss(); showPrintSequenceDialog();
953:         });
954:         panel.addView(save, lp(-1,dp(54)));
955: 
956:         if (!creating && printSequence.hasSplit()) {
957:             Button remove = compactButton("RIMUOVI SPLIT GRADE");
958:             remove.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
959:             remove.setBackground(roundRect(RED,9,0,0));
960:             remove.setOnClickListener(v -> {
961:                 printSequence.split = new SplitGradePlan();
962:                 for (PrintCorrection c : printSequence.corrections) c.phase=PrintCorrection.PHASE_BASE;
963:                 persistPrintSequence(); dialog.dismiss(); showPrintSequenceDialog();
964:             });
965:             panel.addView(remove, margin(lp(-1,dp(48)),0,8,0,0));
966:         }
967:         Button cancel=compactButton("ANNULLA"); cancel.setTextColor(Color.WHITE); cancel.setBackground(roundRect(BUTTON,9,0,0)); cancel.setOnClickListener(v->{dialog.dismiss();showPrintSequenceDialog();}); panel.addView(cancel, margin(lp(-1,dp(48)),0,8,0,0));
968:         dialog.setContentView(sc); Window w=dialog.getWindow(); if(w!=null)w.setBackgroundDrawableResource(android.R.color.transparent); dialog.show();
969:         if(w!=null)w.setLayout((int)(getResources().getDisplayMetrics().widthPixels*0.94f),(int)(getResources().getDisplayMetrics().heightPixels*0.88f));
970:     }

972:     private void showPrintCorrectionEditor(final int index) {
973:         if (darkroomMode || printSequence == null || index < 0 || index >= printSequence.corrections.size()) return;
974:         final PrintCorrection original = printSequence.corrections.get(index);
975:         final boolean creatingCorrection = original.label == null || original.label.trim().isEmpty();
976:         final PrintCorrection c = original.copy();
977:         final Dialog dialog = new Dialog(this);
978:         dialog.requestWindowFeature(Window.FEATURE_NO_TITLE);
979:         LinearLayout panel = new LinearLayout(this);
980:         panel.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
981:         panel.setPadding(dp(18), dp(16), dp(18), dp(18));
982:         panel.setBackground(roundRect(CARD, 14, 1, BORDER));
983: 
984:         final int featureColor = c.isDodge() ? DODGE_BISCAY_BAY : BURN_RUST;
985:         panel.addView(text(c.isDodge() ? "DODGE" : "BURN", 19, featureColor, true), lp(-1, -2));
986:         TextView explain = text(c.isDodge()
987:                 ? "Riduce l’esposizione durante la base. Con Split Grade scegli se applicarlo al giallo, al magenta o a entrambe le fasi."
988:                 : "Aggiunge una nuova esposizione dopo la base. Con Split Grade scegli il filtro del BURN.", 12, MUTED, false);
989:         explain.setPadding(0, dp(4), 0, dp(10)); panel.addView(explain, lp(-1,-2));
990: 
991:         final EditText label = editField(c.isDodge() ? "Zona / maschera — es. Volto" : "Zona — es. Cielo", c.label);
992:         panel.addView(label, margin(lp(-1, dp(52)),0,0,0,10));
993: 
994:         final String[] phase = {printSequence.hasSplit()
995:                 ? (c.isBoth() ? PrintCorrection.PHASE_BOTH : (c.isHard() ? PrintCorrection.PHASE_HARD : PrintCorrection.PHASE_SOFT))
996:                 : PrintCorrection.PHASE_BASE};
997:         final String[] burnMode = {PrintCorrection.normalizeBurnFilter(c.burnFilterMode)};
998:         final int[] burnFilterValue = {PrintCorrection.snap5(c.burnFilterValue)};
999: 
1000:         if (printSequence.hasSplit() && c.isDodge()) {
1001:             panel.addView(text("APPLICA DURANTE",11,MUTED,true), margin(lp(-1,-2),0,0,0,4));
1002:             LinearLayout phases=new LinearLayout(this); phases.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
1003:             final Button soft=compactButton("GIALLO");
1004:             final Button hard=compactButton("MAGENTA");
1005:             final Button both=compactButton("ENTRAMBE");
1006:             final Runnable style=()->{
1007:                 boolean sft=PrintCorrection.PHASE_SOFT.equals(phase[0]);
1008:                 boolean hrd=PrintCorrection.PHASE_HARD.equals(phase[0]);
1009:                 boolean bth=PrintCorrection.PHASE_BOTH.equals(phase[0]);
1010:                 soft.setBackground(roundRect(sft?featureColor:Color.rgb(55,60,64),8,0,0));
1011:                 hard.setBackground(roundRect(hrd?featureColor:Color.rgb(55,60,64),8,0,0));
1012:                 both.setBackground(roundRect(bth?featureColor:Color.rgb(55,60,64),8,0,0));

1159: 
1160:     private boolean validatePrintSequenceForBase() {
1161:         if (printSequence == null || printSequence.isEmpty()) return true;
1162:         if (printSequence.hasSplit()) {
1163:             // I due tempi Split Grade sono esposizioni sperimentali distinte.
1164:             // Non esiste alcun vincolo rispetto al vecchio tempo singolo.
1165:             printSequence.split.sanitize();
1166:         }
1167:         for (PrintCorrection c : printSequence.dodges()) {
1168:             if (printSequence.hasSplit() && c.isBoth()) {
1169:                 int softCue = c.resolvedMs(printSequence.split.softMs);
1170:                 int hardCue = c.resolvedMs(printSequence.split.hardMs);
1171:                 if (softCue >= printSequence.split.softMs || hardCue >= printSequence.split.hardMs) {
1172:                     setStatusPresentation("ATTENZIONE", "DODGE " + c.safeLabel() + ": deve poter terminare prima della fine sia del giallo sia del magenta", RED);
1173:                     return false;
1174:                 }
1175:                 continue;
1176:             }
1177:             int baseMs = printSequence.baseMsFor(c, printWidthMs);
1178:             int cueMs = c.resolvedMs(baseMs);
1179:             if (cueMs >= baseMs) {
1180:                 setStatusPresentation("ATTENZIONE", "DODGE " + c.safeLabel() + ": il cue deve avvenire prima della fine della esposizione " + (printSequence.hasSplit() ? (c.isHard() ? "magenta" : "gialla") : "base"), RED);
1181:                 return false;
1182:             }
1183:         }
1184:         return true;
1185:     }

1160:     private boolean validatePrintSequenceForBase() {
1161:         if (printSequence == null || printSequence.isEmpty()) return true;
1162:         if (printSequence.hasSplit()) {
1163:             // I due tempi Split Grade sono esposizioni sperimentali distinte.
1164:             // Non esiste alcun vincolo rispetto al vecchio tempo singolo.
1165:             printSequence.split.sanitize();
1166:         }
1167:         for (PrintCorrection c : printSequence.dodges()) {
1168:             if (printSequence.hasSplit() && c.isBoth()) {
1169:                 int softCue = c.resolvedMs(printSequence.split.softMs);
1170:                 int hardCue = c.resolvedMs(printSequence.split.hardMs);
1171:                 if (softCue >= printSequence.split.softMs || hardCue >= printSequence.split.hardMs) {
1172:                     setStatusPresentation("ATTENZIONE", "DODGE " + c.safeLabel() + ": deve poter terminare prima della fine sia del giallo sia del magenta", RED);
1173:                     return false;
1174:                 }
1175:                 continue;
1176:             }
1177:             int baseMs = printSequence.baseMsFor(c, printWidthMs);
1178:             int cueMs = c.resolvedMs(baseMs);
1179:             if (cueMs >= baseMs) {
1180:                 setStatusPresentation("ATTENZIONE", "DODGE " + c.safeLabel() + ": il cue deve avvenire prima della fine della esposizione " + (printSequence.hasSplit() ? (c.isHard() ? "magenta" : "gialla") : "base"), RED);
1181:                 return false;
1182:             }
1183:         }
1184:         return true;
1185:     }

1370:     private String recipeOriginalLabel(LogEntry entry, String fallback) {
1371:         ExposureRecipe r=ExposureRecipe.decode(entry==null?"":entry.recipeState);
1372:         return r.hasBase()?r.originalLine():fallback;
1373:     }
1374:     private String recipeOperationalLabel(LogEntry entry, String fallback) {
1375:         ExposureRecipe r=ExposureRecipe.decode(entry==null?"":entry.recipeState);
1376:         return r.hasBase()?r.operationalLine(entry.exposureMs):fallback;
1377:     }
1378:     private String testFilterLabel(LogEntry entry) {
1379:         if(entry==null) return "—";
1380:         String f=ExposureRecipe.filterLabel(entry.testBaseFilterType,entry.testBaseFilterValue);
1381:         return "NESSUNO".equals(f)?"Nessuno":f;
1382:     }

1384:     private boolean isSplitProvino() {
1385:         return provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_SOFT || provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_HARD;
1386:     }
1387: 
1388:     private void persistSplitProvinoState() {
1389:         getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
1390:                 .putInt("provinoFlow", provinoFlow)
1391:                 .putInt("splitProvinoSoftYellow", ExposureRecipe.snap5(splitSoftYellow))
1392:                 .putInt("splitProvinoSoftMs", splitSoftChosenMs <= 0 ? 0 : snap(splitSoftChosenMs, 500, 36_000_000))
1393:                 .putInt("splitProvinoSoftStrip", splitSoftChosenStrip)
1394:                 .putInt("splitProvinoHardMagenta", ExposureRecipe.snap5(splitHardMagenta))
1395:                 .putInt("splitProvinoHardMs", splitHardChosenMs <= 0 ? 0 : snap(splitHardChosenMs, 500, 36_000_000))
1396:                 .putInt("splitProvinoHardStrip", splitHardChosenStrip)
1397:                 .putString("splitProvinoReturnFilterType", ExposureRecipe.normalizeFilter(splitReturnFilterType))
1398:                 .putInt("splitProvinoReturnFilterValue", ExposureRecipe.snap5(splitReturnFilterValue))
1399:                 .putInt("splitProvinoReturnTestWidthMs", snap(splitReturnTestWidthMs, 500, 30_000))
1400:                 .apply();
1401:     }
1402: 
1403:     private void markTestResultHandled(long testAt) {
1404:         if (testAt > 0L) getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putLong("lastTestChooserShownAt", testAt).apply();
1405:         refreshPendingTestStripChoiceUi();
1406:     }
1407: 
1408:     private void markCurrentTestResultHandled() {
1409:         long testAt = getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE).getLong("lastTestAt", 0L);
1410:         markTestResultHandled(testAt);
1411:     }
1412: 
1413:     private void invalidateSplitHardChoice() {
1414:         splitHardChosenMs = 0;
1415:         splitHardChosenStrip = -1;
1416:     }
1417: 
1418:     private void startSplitProvino() {
1419:         if (armed || provinoFlow != PROVINO_SINGLE) {
1420:             if (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_SOFT || provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_HARD) refreshSplitProvinoUi();
1421:             return;
1422:         }
1423:         markCurrentTestResultHandled();
1424:         splitReturnFilterType = testBaseFilterType;
1425:         splitReturnFilterValue = testBaseFilterValue;
1426:         splitReturnTestWidthMs = testWidthMs;
1427:         provinoFlow = PROVINO_SPLIT_SOFT;
1428:         splitSoftYellow = 60;
1429:         splitSoftChosenMs = 0;
1430:         splitSoftChosenStrip = -1;
1431:         splitHardMagenta = 180;
1432:         invalidateSplitHardChoice();
1433:         testBaseFilterType = ExposureRecipe.FILTER_YELLOW;
1434:         testBaseFilterValue = splitSoftYellow;
1435:         persistTestBaseFilter();
1436:         persistSplitProvinoState();
1437:         refreshSplitProvinoUi();
1438:         setStatusPresentation("SPLIT GRADE — FASE 1 DI 2", "Trova sperimentalmente il tempo morbido. Nessuna conversione automatica.", BLUE);
1439:     }
1440: 
1441:     private void requestSingleProvinoMode() {

855:     private void showSplitGradeEditor(final boolean creating) {
856:         if (darkroomMode) return;
857:         if (printSequence == null) printSequence = new PrintSequence();
858:         final SplitGradePlan original = printSequence.split == null ? new SplitGradePlan() : printSequence.split.copy();
859:         final SplitGradePlan draft = original.copy();
860:         draft.enabled = true;
861:         if (creating) {
862:             if (printWidthMs < 1000) {
863:                 Toast.makeText(this, "Per lo Split Grade la base deve essere almeno 1,0 s", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
864:                 return;
865:             }
866:             // Default operativo di riferimento: Fomaspeed Variant 311 RC + testa colore Meopta.
867:             draft.softYellow = 60;
868:             draft.hardMagenta = 180;
869:             int units = Math.max(2, printWidthMs / 500);
870:             int softUnits = (units + 1) / 2;
871:             draft.softMs = softUnits * 500;
872:             draft.hardMs = (units - softUnits) * 500;
873:         }
874:         draft.sanitize();
875:         if (draft.totalMs() > printWidthMs && printWidthMs >= 1000) {
876:             int units = Math.max(2, printWidthMs / 500);
877:             int total = Math.max(1, draft.totalMs());
878:             int softUnits = Math.max(1, Math.min(units - 1, Math.round((draft.softMs / (float) total) * units)));
879:             draft.softMs = softUnits * 500;
880:             draft.hardMs = (units - softUnits) * 500;
881:         }

855:     private void showSplitGradeEditor(final boolean creating) {
856:         if (darkroomMode) return;
857:         if (printSequence == null) printSequence = new PrintSequence();
858:         final SplitGradePlan original = printSequence.split == null ? new SplitGradePlan() : printSequence.split.copy();
859:         final SplitGradePlan draft = original.copy();
860:         draft.enabled = true;
861:         if (creating) {
862:             if (printWidthMs < 1000) {
863:                 Toast.makeText(this, "Per lo Split Grade la base deve essere almeno 1,0 s", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
864:                 return;
865:             }
866:             // Default operativo di riferimento: Fomaspeed Variant 311 RC + testa colore Meopta.
867:             draft.softYellow = 60;
868:             draft.hardMagenta = 180;
869:             int units = Math.max(2, printWidthMs / 500);
870:             int softUnits = (units + 1) / 2;
871:             draft.softMs = softUnits * 500;
872:             draft.hardMs = (units - softUnits) * 500;
873:         }
874:         draft.sanitize();
875:         if (draft.totalMs() > printWidthMs && printWidthMs >= 1000) {
876:             int units = Math.max(2, printWidthMs / 500);
877:             int total = Math.max(1, draft.totalMs());
878:             int softUnits = Math.max(1, Math.min(units - 1, Math.round((draft.softMs / (float) total) * units)));
879:             draft.softMs = softUnits * 500;
880:             draft.hardMs = (units - softUnits) * 500;
881:         }
882: 
883:         final Dialog dialog = new Dialog(this);
884:         dialog.requestWindowFeature(Window.FEATURE_NO_TITLE);
885:         ScrollView sc = new ScrollView(this);
886:         LinearLayout panel = new LinearLayout(this);
887:         panel.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
888:         panel.setPadding(dp(18), dp(16), dp(18), dp(18));
889:         panel.setBackground(roundRect(CARD, 14, 1, BORDER));
890:         sc.addView(panel, new ScrollView.LayoutParams(-1, -2));
891: 
892:         panel.addView(text("SPLIT GRADE", 20, SPLIT_VIVA_MAGENTA, true), lp(-1, -2));
893:         TextView note = text("Foma Variant + Meopta · MORBIDA 60Y / 0M · DURA 0Y / 180M · valori modificabili", 12, MUTED, false);
894:         note.setPadding(0, dp(4), 0, dp(6));
895:         panel.addView(note, lp(-1, -2));
896:         Button splitInfo = compactButton("ⓘ  COME FUNZIONA");
897:         splitInfo.setTextSize(11);
898:         splitInfo.setOnClickListener(v -> showAppConfirmDialog(
899:                 "COME FUNZIONA LO SPLIT GRADE",
900:                 "MORBIDO / GIALLO: agisce soprattutto sulla resa dei toni chiari e produce una risposta a contrasto più basso.\n\n"

872:             draft.hardMs = (units - softUnits) * 500;
873:         }
874:         draft.sanitize();
875:         if (draft.totalMs() > printWidthMs && printWidthMs >= 1000) {
876:             int units = Math.max(2, printWidthMs / 500);
877:             int total = Math.max(1, draft.totalMs());
878:             int softUnits = Math.max(1, Math.min(units - 1, Math.round((draft.softMs / (float) total) * units)));
879:             draft.softMs = softUnits * 500;
880:             draft.hardMs = (units - softUnits) * 500;
881:         }

724:     private void updatePrintSequenceUi() {
725:         if (printSequenceButton == null || printSequenceSummary == null) return;
726:         if (printSequence == null) printSequence = new PrintSequence();
727:         printSequenceButton.setText("PIANO DI STAMPA");
728: 
729:         String base = recipeBaseSummary();
730:         boolean hasRecipe = !base.isEmpty() || !printSequence.isEmpty()
731:                 || (exposureRecipe != null && exposureRecipe.globalQuarterStops != 0);
732:         if (!hasRecipe) {
733:             printSequenceSummary.setText("");
734:             printSequenceSummary.setVisibility(View.GONE);
735:             return;
736:         }
737: 
738:         StringBuilder s = new StringBuilder();
739:         if (!base.isEmpty()) s.append(base);
740:         if (s.length() > 0) s.append("\n\n");
741:         s.append("ESPOSIZIONE\n");
742:         if (printSequence.hasSplit()) {
743:             s.append(printSequence.split.softLine()).append('\n').append(printSequence.split.hardLine());
744:         } else {
745:             s.append("SINGOLA · ").append(formatTime(printWidthMs));
746:             if (exposureRecipe != null && exposureRecipe.hasBase()) {
747:                 String f = exposureRecipe.filterLabel();
748:                 if (!"NESSUNO".equals(f)) s.append(" · ").append(f);
749:                 s.append(" · ").append(exposureRecipe.densityLabel());
750:             }
751:         }
752: 
753:         if (!printSequence.corrections.isEmpty()) {
754:             s.append("\n\nCORREZIONI");
755:             for (PrintCorrection c : printSequence.corrections) {
756:                 if (c == null) continue;
757:                 int baseMs = printSequence.baseMsFor(c, printWidthMs);
758:                 s.append('\n').append(c.displayLine(baseMs, printSequence.hasSplit()));
759:             }
760:         }
761:         if (exposureRecipe != null && exposureRecipe.globalQuarterStops != 0)
762:             s.append("\n\nCORREZIONE GLOBALE · ").append(exposureRecipe.globalLabel());
763:         printSequenceSummary.setText(s.toString());
764:         printSequenceSummary.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);

===== updatePrintSequenceUi =====
326:         ensureSafelightIdleOn();
327:     }

329:     @Override protected void onResume() {
330:         super.onResume();
331:         SharedPreferences ep = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE);
332:         if (ep.getBoolean("enlargementReloadPending", false)) {
333:             printWidthMs = ep.getInt("printWidthMs", printWidthMs);
334:             exposureRecipe = ExposureRecipe.decode(ep.getString("exposureRecipe", ""));
335:             printSequence = PrintSequence.decode(ep.getString("printSequence", ""));
336:             if (exposureRecipe != null && exposureRecipe.hasBase()) {
337:                 testBaseFilterType = ExposureRecipe.normalizeFilter(exposureRecipe.filterType);
338:                 testBaseFilterValue = ExposureRecipe.snap5(exposureRecipe.filterValue);
339:             }
340:             mode = MODE_PRINT;
341:             ep.edit().remove("enlargementReloadPending").putInt("mode", MODE_PRINT).apply();
342:             if (printTimeText != null) printTimeText.setText(formatTime(printWidthMs));
343:             refreshTestBaseFilterUi();
344:             updatePrintSequenceUi();
345:             applyModeUi();
346:         }
347:         // A cycle may have completed while the screen/activity was stopped.
348:         // Re-read the service's durable state instead of relying on a missed broadcast.
349:         restoreRuntimeState();
350:         refreshPendingTestStripChoiceUi();
351:         new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed(this::maybeShowTestResultChooser, 320L);
352: 
353:         if (pendingDarkroomAfterDndPermission) {
354:             pendingDarkroomAfterDndPermission = false;
355:             if (hasDndAccess()) {
356:                 getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putBoolean("darkroomMode", true).apply();
357:                 recreate();
358:                 return;
359:             }
360:             Toast.makeText(this, "Protezione notifiche non autorizzata", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
361:         }
362:         syncDarkroomProtection();
363:     }

365:     @Override protected void onStop() {
366:         activityStarted = false;
367:         reconnectHandler.removeCallbacks(reconnectRunnable);
368:         if (discovery != null) discovery.stop();
369:         discovery = null;
370:         try { unregisterReceiver(stateReceiver); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
371:         super.onStop();
372:     }
373: 
374:     @Override protected void onDestroy() {
375:         statusResetHandler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null);
376:         io.shutdownNow();
377:         super.onDestroy();
378:     }
379: 
380:     private void restoreRuntimeState() {
381:         applyRuntimeState(
382:                 SonoffArmService.loadLastState(this),
383:                 SonoffArmService.loadLastMessage(this));
384:     }
385: 
386:     private void applyRuntimeState(String state, String message) {
387:         if (state == null || state.trim().isEmpty()) state = SonoffArmService.STATE_NORMAL;
388: 
389:         boolean blankNormalRefresh = SonoffArmService.STATE_NORMAL.equals(state)
390:                 && (message == null || message.trim().isEmpty());
391:         if (blankNormalRefresh && android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis() < transientCompletionUntilMs) {
392:             return;
393:         }
394: 
395:         statusResetHandler.removeCallbacks(readyResetRunnable);
396:         if (!SonoffArmService.STATE_NORMAL.equals(state)) transientCompletionUntilMs = 0L;
397: 
398:         boolean cancellable = SonoffArmService.STATE_ARMED.equals(state)
399:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_ARMING.equals(state)
400:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_EXPOSING.equals(state)
401:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_PAUSING.equals(state)
402:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_WAITING_BURN.equals(state)
403:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_WAITING_SPLIT.equals(state);
404:         boolean busy = cancellable || SonoffArmService.STATE_DISARMING.equals(state);
405: 
406:         if (busy) {
407:             armed = true;
408:             setControlsEnabled(false);
409:             normalButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);
410:         } else if (SonoffArmService.STATE_ERROR.equals(state)) {
411:             armed = false;
412:             setControlsEnabled(device != null && device.isValid());
413:             normalButton.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
414:             normalButton.setEnabled(device != null && device.isValid());
415:             normalButton.setAlpha(normalButton.isEnabled() ? 1f : (darkroomMode ? 0.62f : 0.45f));
416:             actionButton.setVisibility(mode == MODE_LOG ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
417:             actionButton.setText("RIPROVA");
418:             actionButton.setEnabled(device != null && device.isValid());
419:             actionButton.setAlpha(actionButton.isEnabled() ? 1f : (darkroomMode ? 0.62f : 0.45f));
420:             setStatusPresentation("ATTENZIONE", message == null ? "Errore del ciclo" : message, RED);
421:             cancelCycleButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);
422:             // An exposure error must not disable the independent manual ON/OFF safelight interlock.
423:             ensureSafelightIdleOn();
424:             return;
425:         } else {
426:             armed = false;
427:             normalButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);
428:             setControlsEnabled(device != null && device.isValid());
429:         }
430: 
431:         cancelCycleButton.setVisibility(cancellable ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
432:         if (!SonoffArmService.STATE_DISARMING.equals(state)) cancelCycleButton.setEnabled(true);
433:         actionButton.setVisibility(mode == MODE_LOG || cancellable || SonoffArmService.STATE_DISARMING.equals(state) ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
434: 
435:         String detail = message == null ? "" : message.trim();
436:         int accent = MUTED;
437:         String title = "PRONTO";
438:         boolean transientCompletion = false;
702:         for (int s : secs) {
703:             Button b = shortcutButton(s + " s", GREEN);
704:             GridLayout.LayoutParams gp = new GridLayout.LayoutParams();
705:             gp.width = dp(74);
706:             gp.height = dp(46);
707:             gp.setMargins(dp(4), dp(4), dp(4), dp(4));
708:             grid.addView(b, gp);
709:             b.setOnClickListener(v -> setPrintTime(s * 1000));
710:         }
711:         box.addView(grid, lp(-1, -2));
712: 
713:         printSequenceButton = compactButton("PIANO DI STAMPA");
714:         printSequenceButton.setOnClickListener(v -> showPrintSequenceDialog());
715:         box.addView(printSequenceButton, margin(lp(-1, dp(50)), 0, 12, 0, 0));
716:         printSequenceSummary = text("", 12, darkroomMode ? RED : AMBER, false);
717:         printSequenceSummary.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
718:         printSequenceSummary.setPadding(dp(6), dp(6), dp(6), 0);
719:         box.addView(printSequenceSummary, lp(-1, -2));
720:         updatePrintSequenceUi();

723: 
724:     private void updatePrintSequenceUi() {
725:         if (printSequenceButton == null || printSequenceSummary == null) return;
726:         if (printSequence == null) printSequence = new PrintSequence();
727:         printSequenceButton.setText("PIANO DI STAMPA");
728: 
729:         String base = recipeBaseSummary();
730:         boolean hasRecipe = !base.isEmpty() || !printSequence.isEmpty()
731:                 || (exposureRecipe != null && exposureRecipe.globalQuarterStops != 0);
732:         if (!hasRecipe) {
733:             printSequenceSummary.setText("");
734:             printSequenceSummary.setVisibility(View.GONE);
735:             return;
736:         }
737: 
738:         StringBuilder s = new StringBuilder();
739:         if (!base.isEmpty()) s.append(base);
740:         if (s.length() > 0) s.append("\n\n");
741:         s.append("ESPOSIZIONE\n");
742:         if (printSequence.hasSplit()) {
743:             s.append(printSequence.split.softLine()).append('\n').append(printSequence.split.hardLine());
744:         } else {
745:             s.append("SINGOLA · ").append(formatTime(printWidthMs));
746:             if (exposureRecipe != null && exposureRecipe.hasBase()) {
747:                 String f = exposureRecipe.filterLabel();
748:                 if (!"NESSUNO".equals(f)) s.append(" · ").append(f);
749:                 s.append(" · ").append(exposureRecipe.densityLabel());
750:             }
751:         }
752: 
753:         if (!printSequence.corrections.isEmpty()) {
754:             s.append("\n\nCORREZIONI");
755:             for (PrintCorrection c : printSequence.corrections) {
756:                 if (c == null) continue;
757:                 int baseMs = printSequence.baseMsFor(c, printWidthMs);
758:                 s.append('\n').append(c.displayLine(baseMs, printSequence.hasSplit()));
759:             }
760:         }
761:         if (exposureRecipe != null && exposureRecipe.globalQuarterStops != 0)
762:             s.append("\n\nCORREZIONE GLOBALE · ").append(exposureRecipe.globalLabel());
763:         printSequenceSummary.setText(s.toString());
764:         printSequenceSummary.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
765:     }

767:     private void persistPrintSequence() {
768:         if (printSequence == null) printSequence = new PrintSequence();
769:         getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putString("printSequence", printSequence.encode()).apply();
770:         updatePrintSequenceUi();
771:         applyModeUi();
772:     }

1263:         if (exposureRecipe == null) exposureRecipe = new ExposureRecipe();

1268:     private boolean canLengthenTimes() {
1269:         SharedPreferences s = getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE);
1270:         long lastPrintAt = s.getLong("lastPrintAt", 0L);
1271:         if (lastPrintAt <= 0L) return false;
1272:         long chosenAt = exposureRecipe == null ? 0L : Math.max(0L, exposureRecipe.baseChosenAt);
1273:         return chosenAt <= 0L || lastPrintAt >= chosenAt;
1274:     }
1275: 
1276:     private void scaleWholeRecipe(int quarterStopDelta) {
1277:         if (quarterStopDelta == 0) return;
1278:         ensureExposureRecipeBase();
1279:         int oldBase = printWidthMs;
1280:         int newBase = ExposureRecipe.scaledMs(oldBase, quarterStopDelta);
1281:         if (printSequence != null) {
1282:             if (printSequence.hasSplit()) {
1283:                 printSequence.split.softMs = ExposureRecipe.scaledMs(printSequence.split.softMs, quarterStopDelta);
1284:                 printSequence.split.hardMs = ExposureRecipe.scaledMs(printSequence.split.hardMs, quarterStopDelta);
1285:                 printSequence.split.sanitize();
1286:             }
1287:             for (PrintCorrection c : printSequence.corrections) {
1288:                 if (c == null || c.quarterStops > 0) continue;
1289:                 c.milliseconds = ExposureRecipe.scaledMs(c.milliseconds, quarterStopDelta);
1290:             }
1291:         }
1292:         exposureRecipe.operationalBaseMs = newBase;
1293:         printWidthMs = newBase;
1294:         getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
1295:                 .putInt("printWidthMs", printWidthMs)
1296:                 .putString("printSequence", printSequence == null ? "" : printSequence.encode())
1297:                 .putString("exposureRecipe", exposureRecipe.encode()).apply();
1298:         if (printTimeText != null) printTimeText.setText(formatTime(printWidthMs));
1299:         updatePrintSequenceUi();
1300:         applyModeUi();
1301:     }

1303:     private String recipeBaseSummary() {
1304:         if (exposureRecipe == null || !exposureRecipe.hasBase()) return "";
1305:         if (printSequence != null && printSequence.hasSplit()) {
1306:             return "BASE DI PARTENZA · " + exposureRecipe.originalLine()
1307:                     + (exposureRecipe.densityQuarterSteps > 0 ? "\nDENSITÀ OPERATIVA · " + exposureRecipe.densityLabel() + " · applicata alla ricetta finale" : "");
1308:         }
1309:         String s = "BASE · " + exposureRecipe.operationalLine(printWidthMs);
1310:         if (exposureRecipe.originalBaseMs > 0 && (exposureRecipe.originalBaseMs != exposureRecipe.operationalBaseMs || exposureRecipe.densityQuarterSteps > 0))
1311:             s = "BASE ORIGINALE · " + exposureRecipe.originalLine() + "\nBASE OPERATIVA · " + exposureRecipe.operationalLine(printWidthMs);
1312:         return s;
1313:     }

1371:         ExposureRecipe r=ExposureRecipe.decode(entry==null?"":entry.recipeState);

1374:     private String recipeOperationalLabel(LogEntry entry, String fallback) {
1375:         ExposureRecipe r=ExposureRecipe.decode(entry==null?"":entry.recipeState);
1376:         return r.hasBase()?r.operationalLine(entry.exposureMs):fallback;

2142:     private void useLogEntryForPrint(LogEntry entry) {
2143:         if (entry == null || entry.exposureMs <= 0) return;
2144:         exposureRecipe = ExposureRecipe.decode(entry.recipeState);
2145:         if (!exposureRecipe.hasBase()) { exposureRecipe.originalBaseMs=entry.exposureMs; exposureRecipe.operationalBaseMs=entry.exposureMs; exposureRecipe.filterType=entry.testBaseFilterType; exposureRecipe.filterValue=entry.testBaseFilterValue; }
2146:         exposureRecipe.baseChosenAt = System.currentTimeMillis();
2147:         setPrintTime(exposureRecipe.operationalBaseMs > 0 ? exposureRecipe.operationalBaseMs : entry.exposureMs);
2148:         printSequence = PrintSequence.decode(entry.printSequence);
2149:         testBaseFilterType = ExposureRecipe.normalizeFilter(entry.testBaseFilterType);
2150:         testBaseFilterValue = ExposureRecipe.snap5(entry.testBaseFilterValue);
2151:         getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
2152:                 .putString("exposureRecipe", exposureRecipe.encode())
2153:                 .putString("testBaseFilterType", testBaseFilterType)
2154:                 .putInt("testBaseFilterValue", testBaseFilterValue)
2155:                 .putString("enlargementMeta", entry.enlargementMeta == null ? "" : entry.enlargementMeta)
2156:                 .apply();
2157:         refreshTestBaseFilterUi();
2158:         getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putString("printSequence", printSequence.encode()).apply();
2159:         updatePrintSequenceUi();
2160:         getSharedPreferences("log_reprint", MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
2161:                 .clear()
2162:                 .putBoolean("active", true)
2163:                 .putLong("activatedAt", System.currentTimeMillis())
2164:                 .putString("title", entry.title == null ? "" : entry.title)
2165:                 .putString("negative", entry.negative == null ? "" : entry.negative)
2166:                 .putString("aperture", entry.aperture == null ? "" : entry.aperture)
2167:                 .putString("columnHeight", entry.columnHeight == null ? "" : entry.columnHeight)
2168:                 .putString("magenta", entry.magenta == null ? "" : entry.magenta)

2172:                 .putString("notes", entry.notes == null ? "" : entry.notes)
2173:                 .putString("printSequence", entry.printSequence == null ? "" : entry.printSequence)

2205:     private static String enlargementMetaValue(String meta, String key) {
2206:         if (meta == null || meta.trim().isEmpty()) return "";
2207:         for (String part : meta.split("\\|")) {
2208:             if (part.startsWith(key + "=")) return part.substring(key.length() + 1);
2209:         }
2210:         return "";
2211:     }

2213:     private static boolean applyEnlargementSnapshotToVisibleLogFields(LogEntry entry) {
2214:         if (entry == null || entry.enlargementMeta == null || entry.enlargementMeta.trim().isEmpty()) return false;
2215:         boolean changed = false;
2216:         String col = enlargementMetaValue(entry.enlargementMeta, "col");
2217:         if (!col.isEmpty()) {
2218:             try {
2219:                 String value = String.format(Locale.ITALY, "%.1f", Double.parseDouble(col));
2220:                 if (entry.columnHeight == null || !value.equals(entry.columnHeight.trim())) {
2221:                     entry.columnHeight = value;
2222:                     changed = true;
2223:                 }
2224:             } catch (Exception ignored) {}
2225:         }
2226:         String paper = enlargementMetaValue(entry.enlargementMeta, "paper");
2227:         if (!paper.isEmpty()) {
2228:             String format = paper.replace('.', ',').replace("x", " × ") + " cm";
2229:             String current = entry.paper == null ? "" : entry.paper.trim();
2230:             if (current.isEmpty()) {
2231:                 entry.paper = format;
2232:                 changed = true;
2233:             } else if (!current.contains(format) && !current.contains(paper)) {
2234:                 entry.paper = current + " · " + format;
2235:                 changed = true;
2236:             }
2237:         }
2238:         return changed;
2239:     }

3063:     private void startServiceCompat(Intent intent) {
3064:         if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) {

3066:                 java.lang.reflect.Method m = Context.class.getMethod("startForegroundService", Intent.class);
3067:                 m.invoke(this, intent);

3070:         }

3153:     private void showSafelightPicker() {

3156:             if (!f.diyCandidate) continue;

3161:         if (list.isEmpty()) {
3162:             showAppConfirmDialog("NESSUN SECONDO SONOFF DIY TROVATO",
3163:                     "La luce rossa richiede un secondo SONOFF in modalità DIY, diverso da quello dell'ingranditore. Attendi la ricerca di rete e riprova.",
3164:                     "OK", null, null);
3165:             return;
3166:         }
3167:         String[] labels = new String[list.size()];
3168:         for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {
3169:             FoundDevice f = list.get(i);
3170:             String selected = f.config.deviceId.equals(selectedSafelightDeviceId) ? "  ✓" : "";
3171:             labels[i] = "ID " + f.config.deviceId + selected + "\n" + f.config.host + ":" + f.config.port + " • DIY";
3172:         }
3173:         showAppChoiceDialog("SCEGLI IL SONOFF DELLA LUCE ROSSA", labels,
3174:                 which -> selectSafelight(list.get(which)), "ANNULLA");
3175:     }

3177:     private void selectSafelight(FoundDevice found) {
3178:         if (found == null || !found.diyCandidate || !found.config.isValid()) return;
3179:         if (found.config.deviceId.equals(selectedDeviceId)) {
3180:             Toast.makeText(this, "Scegli un SONOFF diverso da quello dell’ingranditore", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
3181:             return;
3182:         }
3183:         selectedSafelightDeviceId = found.config.deviceId;
3184:         safelightDevice = found.config;
3185:         SafelightConfig.save(this, found.config);
3186:         updateSafelightStatus();
3187:         if (safelightAuto) ensureSafelightIdleOn();
3188:         Toast.makeText(this, "SONOFF luce rossa selezionato", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
3189:     }

1159: 
1160:     private boolean validatePrintSequenceForBase() {
1161:         if (printSequence == null || printSequence.isEmpty()) return true;
1162:         if (printSequence.hasSplit()) {
1163:             // I due tempi Split Grade sono esposizioni sperimentali distinte.
1164:             // Non esiste alcun vincolo rispetto al vecchio tempo singolo.
1165:             printSequence.split.sanitize();
1166:         }
1167:         for (PrintCorrection c : printSequence.dodges()) {
1168:             if (printSequence.hasSplit() && c.isBoth()) {
1169:                 int softCue = c.resolvedMs(printSequence.split.softMs);
1170:                 int hardCue = c.resolvedMs(printSequence.split.hardMs);
1171:                 if (softCue >= printSequence.split.softMs || hardCue >= printSequence.split.hardMs) {
1172:                     setStatusPresentation("ATTENZIONE", "DODGE " + c.safeLabel() + ": deve poter terminare prima della fine sia del giallo sia del magenta", RED);
1173:                     return false;
1174:                 }
1175:                 continue;
1176:             }
1177:             int baseMs = printSequence.baseMsFor(c, printWidthMs);
1178:             int cueMs = c.resolvedMs(baseMs);
1179:             if (cueMs >= baseMs) {
1180:                 setStatusPresentation("ATTENZIONE", "DODGE " + c.safeLabel() + ": il cue deve avvenire prima della fine della esposizione " + (printSequence.hasSplit() ? (c.isHard() ? "magenta" : "gialla") : "base"), RED);
1181:                 return false;
1182:             }
1183:         }
1184:         return true;
1185:     }

2841:     private boolean hasPendingTestStripChoice() {
2842:         SharedPreferences session = getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE);
2843:         long testAt = session.getLong("lastTestAt", 0L);
2844:         if (testAt <= 0L) return false;
2845:         long chosenAt = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).getLong("lastTestChooserShownAt", 0L);
2846:         return chosenAt < testAt;
2847:     }

386:     private void applyRuntimeState(String state, String message) {
387:         if (state == null || state.trim().isEmpty()) state = SonoffArmService.STATE_NORMAL;
388: 
389:         boolean blankNormalRefresh = SonoffArmService.STATE_NORMAL.equals(state)
390:                 && (message == null || message.trim().isEmpty());
391:         if (blankNormalRefresh && android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis() < transientCompletionUntilMs) {
392:             return;
393:         }
394: 
395:         statusResetHandler.removeCallbacks(readyResetRunnable);
396:         if (!SonoffArmService.STATE_NORMAL.equals(state)) transientCompletionUntilMs = 0L;
397: 
398:         boolean cancellable = SonoffArmService.STATE_ARMED.equals(state)
399:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_ARMING.equals(state)
400:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_EXPOSING.equals(state)
401:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_PAUSING.equals(state)
402:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_WAITING_BURN.equals(state)
403:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_WAITING_SPLIT.equals(state);
404:         boolean busy = cancellable || SonoffArmService.STATE_DISARMING.equals(state);
405: 
406:         if (busy) {
407:             armed = true;
408:             setControlsEnabled(false);
409:             normalButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);
410:         } else if (SonoffArmService.STATE_ERROR.equals(state)) {
411:             armed = false;
412:             setControlsEnabled(device != null && device.isValid());
413:             normalButton.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
414:             normalButton.setEnabled(device != null && device.isValid());
415:             normalButton.setAlpha(normalButton.isEnabled() ? 1f : (darkroomMode ? 0.62f : 0.45f));
416:             actionButton.setVisibility(mode == MODE_LOG ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
417:             actionButton.setText("RIPROVA");
418:             actionButton.setEnabled(device != null && device.isValid());
419:             actionButton.setAlpha(actionButton.isEnabled() ? 1f : (darkroomMode ? 0.62f : 0.45f));
420:             setStatusPresentation("ATTENZIONE", message == null ? "Errore del ciclo" : message, RED);
421:             cancelCycleButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);
422:             // An exposure error must not disable the independent manual ON/OFF safelight interlock.
423:             ensureSafelightIdleOn();
424:             return;
425:         } else {
426:             armed = false;
427:             normalButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);
428:             setControlsEnabled(device != null && device.isValid());
429:         }
430: 
431:         cancelCycleButton.setVisibility(cancellable ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
432:         if (!SonoffArmService.STATE_DISARMING.equals(state)) cancelCycleButton.setEnabled(true);
433:         actionButton.setVisibility(mode == MODE_LOG || cancellable || SonoffArmService.STATE_DISARMING.equals(state) ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
434: 
435:         String detail = message == null ? "" : message.trim();
436:         int accent = MUTED;
437:         String title = "PRONTO";
438:         boolean transientCompletion = false;
439:         if (SonoffArmService.STATE_ARMING.equals(state)) {
440:             title = "PREPARAZIONE";
441:             accent = AMBER;
442:         } else if (SonoffArmService.STATE_ARMED.equals(state)) {
443:             title = "ARMATO";
444:             accent = mode == MODE_TEST ? BLUE : GREEN;
445:         } else if (SonoffArmService.STATE_EXPOSING.equals(state)) {
464:                 title = "✓  STAMPA COMPLETATA — " + formatTime(ms);
465:                 detail = "Inching disattivato • pronto per una nuova esposizione";
466:                 accent = GREEN;
467:                 transientCompletion = true;
468:             } else if (detail.toLowerCase(Locale.ITALY).contains("provino completato")) {
469:                 int countDone = session.getInt("lastTestCount", testCount);
470:                 if (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_SOFT) {
471:                     title = "✓  FASE 1 COMPLETATA — MORBIDO";
472:                     detail = "Scegli il tempo morbido oppure reimposta la fase";
473:                 } else if (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_HARD) {
474:                     title = "✓  FASE 2 COMPLETATA — DURO";
475:                     detail = "Ogni striscia comprende già la base morbida scelta";
476:                 } else {
477:                     title = "✓  PROVINO COMPLETATO — " + countDone + "/" + countDone;
478:                     detail = "Scegli la striscia da usare come punto di partenza per la stampa";
479:                 }
480:                 accent = BLUE;
481:                 transientCompletion = true;

484:                 title = "CICLO ANNULLATO";

492:                     accent = GREEN;

496:                     accent = darkroomMode ? RED : AMBER;
497:                 }
498:             }
499:         }

501:         if (transientCompletion) {
502:             transientCompletionUntilMs = android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis() + 6000L;
503:             statusResetHandler.postDelayed(readyResetRunnable, 6000L);
504:         }

509:             saveLogButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);

2841:     private boolean hasPendingTestStripChoice() {
2842:         SharedPreferences session = getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE);
2843:         long testAt = session.getLong("lastTestAt", 0L);
2844:         if (testAt <= 0L) return false;
2845:         long chosenAt = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).getLong("lastTestChooserShownAt", 0L);
2846:         return chosenAt < testAt;
2847:     }
2848: 
2849:     private void refreshPendingTestStripChoiceUi() {
2850:         if (testPendingChoiceButton == null) return;
2851:         boolean pending = hasPendingTestStripChoice();
2852:         testPendingChoiceButton.setVisibility(pending ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
2853:         if (pending) testPendingChoiceButton.setText(provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_SOFT ? "SCEGLI IL TEMPO MORBIDO" : (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_HARD ? "SCEGLI IL TEMPO DURO" : "SCEGLI STRISCIA DEL PROVINO"));
2854:         testPendingChoiceButton.setEnabled(pending && !armed);
2855:         testPendingChoiceButton.setAlpha(testPendingChoiceButton.isEnabled() ? 1f : (darkroomMode ? 0.62f : 0.45f));
2856:     }

2858:     private void maybeShowTestResultChooser() {
2859:         maybeShowTestResultChooser(false);
2860:     }
2861: 
2862:     private void maybeShowTestResultChooser(boolean forceManual) {
2863:         if (armed || mode != MODE_TEST || isFinishing()) return;
2864:         if (forceManual) testChooserOpen = false;
2865:         else if (testChooserOpen) return;
2866:         if (!hasWindowFocus()) {
2867:             new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed(() -> maybeShowTestResultChooser(forceManual), 450L);
2868:             return;
2869:         }
2870:         SharedPreferences session = getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE);
2871:         long testAt = session.getLong("lastTestAt", 0L);
2872:         if (testAt <= 0) return;
2873:         SharedPreferences ui = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE);
2874:         if (ui.getLong("lastTestChooserShownAt", 0L) >= testAt) return;
2875: 
2876:         final int step = session.getInt("lastTestMs", testWidthMs);
2877:         final int n = Math.max(2, Math.min(20, session.getInt("lastTestCount", testCount)));
2878:         int[] stored = TimingMath.fromCsv(session.getString("lastTestStripTimes", ""));
2879:         final int[] ascending = stored.length == n ? stored : TimingMath.cumulativeSeries(session.getString("lastTestMethod", TimingMath.METHOD_SECONDS), step, n);
2880:         final String masking = TimingMath.normalizeMaskingMethod(session.getString("lastTestStripMethod", testStripMethod));
2881:         final int[] physical = TimingMath.physicalTargets(ascending, masking);
2882:         final String filterType = ExposureRecipe.normalizeFilter(session.getString("lastTestBaseFilterType", ExposureRecipe.FILTER_NONE));
2883:         final int filterValue = ExposureRecipe.snap5(session.getInt("lastTestBaseFilterValue", 0));
2884:         testChooserOpen = true;
2885:         showProvinoResultDialog(testAt, physical, filterType, filterValue);
2886:     }

2888:     private void showProvinoResultDialog(final long testAt, final int[] physical, final String filterType, final int filterValue) {
2889:         final Dialog dialog = new Dialog(this);
2890:         dialog.requestWindowFeature(Window.FEATURE_NO_TITLE);
2891:         LinearLayout panel = new LinearLayout(this);
2892:         panel.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
2893:         panel.setPadding(dp(18), dp(16), dp(18), dp(18));
2894:         panel.setBackground(roundRect(darkroomMode ? Color.BLACK : CARD, 14, 1, BORDER));
2895: 
2896:         String title;
2897:         String help;
2898:         if (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_SOFT) {
2899:             title = "SCEGLI IL TEMPO MORBIDO";
2900:             help = "Quale striscia restituisce la resa desiderata soprattutto nei toni chiari?";
2901:         } else if (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_HARD) {
2902:             title = "SCEGLI IL TEMPO DURO";
2903:             help = "Ogni striscia comprende già l’esposizione morbida scelta. Quale combinazione produce il miglior equilibrio di ombre e neri?";
2904:         } else {
2905:             title = "PROVINO COMPLETATO — SCEGLI LA STRISCIA";
2906:             help = "Scegli una striscia valida oppure reimposta il provino senza creare una stampa.";
2907:         }
2908:         panel.addView(text(title, 18, darkroomMode ? RED : TEXT_PRIMARY, true), lp(-1,-2));
2909:         TextView note = text(help, 13, MUTED, false);
2910:         note.setPadding(0, dp(5), 0, dp(10)); panel.addView(note, lp(-1,-2));
2911: 
2912:         final int[] selected = {-1};
2913:         final TextView selectedText = text("Nessuna striscia selezionata", 12, BLUE, true);
2914:         selectedText.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);

2916:         panel.addView(selectedText, lp(-1,-2));

2921:             option.setOnClickListener(v -> {
2922:                 selected[0]=idx;
2923:                 selectedText.setText("SELEZIONATA · "+(idx+1)+"ª · "+formatTime(physical[idx]));
2924:             });
2925:             panel.addView(option, margin(lp(-1,dp(47)),0,0,0,6));
2926:         }
2927: 
2928:         if (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SINGLE) {
2929:             Button choose=compactButton("SCEGLI LA STRISCIA");
2930:             choose.setBackground(roundRect(BLUE,9,0,0)); choose.setTextColor(Color.BLACK);
2931:             choose.setOnClickListener(v -> {
2932:                 if(selected[0]<0){Toast.makeText(this,"Seleziona prima una striscia",Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();return;}
2933:                 int imported=snap(physical[selected[0]],500,36_000_000);
2934:                 markTestResultHandled(testAt);
2935:                 exposureRecipe=new ExposureRecipe();
2936:                 exposureRecipe.originalBaseMs=imported;
2937:                 exposureRecipe.operationalBaseMs=imported;
2938:                 exposureRecipe.filterType=filterType;
2939:                 exposureRecipe.filterValue=filterValue;
2940:                 exposureRecipe.densityQuarterSteps=0;
2941:                 exposureRecipe.globalQuarterStops=0;
2942:                 exposureRecipe.baseChosenAt=System.currentTimeMillis();
2943:                 printSequence=new PrintSequence();
2944:                 getSharedPreferences("ui",MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
2945:                         .putString("exposureRecipe",exposureRecipe.encode())
2946:                         .putString("printSequence","").apply();
2947:                 dialog.dismiss();
2948:                 updatePrintSequenceUi();
2949:                 setMode(MODE_PRINT);
2950:                 setPrintTime(imported);
2951:                 setStatusPresentation("DAL PROVINO — "+formatTime(imported)+("NESSUNO".equals(filterLabel)?"":" · "+filterLabel),
2952:                         "Tempo e filtrazione trasferiti alla stampa.",GREEN);

2846:         return chosenAt < testAt;

2862:     private void maybeShowTestResultChooser(boolean forceManual) {
2863:         if (armed || mode != MODE_TEST || isFinishing()) return;
2864:         if (forceManual) testChooserOpen = false;
2865:         else if (testChooserOpen) return;
2866:         if (!hasWindowFocus()) {
2867:             new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed(() -> maybeShowTestResultChooser(forceManual), 450L);
2868:             return;
2869:         }
2870:         SharedPreferences session = getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE);
2871:         long testAt = session.getLong("lastTestAt", 0L);
2872:         if (testAt <= 0) return;
2873:         SharedPreferences ui = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE);
2874:         if (ui.getLong("lastTestChooserShownAt", 0L) >= testAt) return;
2875: 
2876:         final int step = session.getInt("lastTestMs", testWidthMs);
2877:         final int n = Math.max(2, Math.min(20, session.getInt("lastTestCount", testCount)));
2878:         int[] stored = TimingMath.fromCsv(session.getString("lastTestStripTimes", ""));
2879:         final int[] ascending = stored.length == n ? stored : TimingMath.cumulativeSeries(session.getString("lastTestMethod", TimingMath.METHOD_SECONDS), step, n);
2880:         final String masking = TimingMath.normalizeMaskingMethod(session.getString("lastTestStripMethod", testStripMethod));
2881:         final int[] physical = TimingMath.physicalTargets(ascending, masking);
2882:         final String filterType = ExposureRecipe.normalizeFilter(session.getString("lastTestBaseFilterType", ExposureRecipe.FILTER_NONE));
2883:         final int filterValue = ExposureRecipe.snap5(session.getInt("lastTestBaseFilterValue", 0));
2884:         testChooserOpen = true;
2885:         showProvinoResultDialog(testAt, physical, filterType, filterValue);
2886:     }

2877:         final int n = Math.max(2, Math.min(20, session.getInt("lastTestCount", testCount)));
2878:         int[] stored = TimingMath.fromCsv(session.getString("lastTestStripTimes", ""));
2879:         final int[] ascending = stored.length == n ? stored : TimingMath.cumulativeSeries(session.getString("lastTestMethod", TimingMath.METHOD_SECONDS), step, n);
2880:         final String masking = TimingMath.normalizeMaskingMethod(session.getString("lastTestStripMethod", testStripMethod));
2881:         final int[] physical = TimingMath.physicalTargets(ascending, masking);
2882:         final String filterType = ExposureRecipe.normalizeFilter(session.getString("lastTestBaseFilterType", ExposureRecipe.FILTER_NONE));
2883:         final int filterValue = ExposureRecipe.snap5(session.getInt("lastTestBaseFilterValue", 0));
2884:         testChooserOpen = true;

2849:     private void refreshPendingTestStripChoiceUi() {
2850:         if (testPendingChoiceButton == null) return;
2851:         boolean pending = hasPendingTestStripChoice();
2852:         testPendingChoiceButton.setVisibility(pending ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
2853:         if (pending) testPendingChoiceButton.setText(provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_SOFT ? "SCEGLI IL TEMPO MORBIDO" : (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_HARD ? "SCEGLI IL TEMPO DURO" : "SCEGLI STRISCIA DEL PROVINO"));
2854:         testPendingChoiceButton.setEnabled(pending && !armed);
2855:         testPendingChoiceButton.setAlpha(testPendingChoiceButton.isEnabled() ? 1f : (darkroomMode ? 0.62f : 0.45f));
2856:     }

2858:     private void maybeShowTestResultChooser() {
2859:         maybeShowTestResultChooser(false);
2860:     }
2861: 
2862:     private void maybeShowTestResultChooser(boolean forceManual) {
2863:         if (armed || mode != MODE_TEST || isFinishing()) return;
2864:         if (forceManual) testChooserOpen = false;
2865:         else if (testChooserOpen) return;
2866:         if (!hasWindowFocus()) {
2867:             new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed(() -> maybeShowTestResultChooser(forceManual), 450L);
2868:             return;
2869:         }
2870:         SharedPreferences session = getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE);
2871:         long testAt = session.getLong("lastTestAt", 0L);
2872:         if (testAt <= 0) return;
2873:         SharedPreferences ui = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE);
2874:         if (ui.getLong("lastTestChooserShownAt", 0L) >= testAt) return;
2875: 
2876:         final int step = session.getInt("lastTestMs", testWidthMs);
2877:         final int n = Math.max(2, Math.min(20, session.getInt("lastTestCount", testCount)));
2878:         int[] stored = TimingMath.fromCsv(session.getString("lastTestStripTimes", ""));
2879:         final int[] ascending = stored.length == n ? stored : TimingMath.cumulativeSeries(session.getString("lastTestMethod", TimingMath.METHOD_SECONDS), step, n);
2880:         final String masking = TimingMath.normalizeMaskingMethod(session.getString("lastTestStripMethod", testStripMethod));
2881:         final int[] physical = TimingMath.physicalTargets(ascending, masking);
2882:         final String filterType = ExposureRecipe.normalizeFilter(session.getString("lastTestBaseFilterType", ExposureRecipe.FILTER_NONE));
2883:         final int filterValue = ExposureRecipe.snap5(session.getInt("lastTestBaseFilterValue", 0));
2884:         testChooserOpen = true;
2885:         showProvinoResultDialog(testAt, physical, filterType, filterValue);
2886:     }

2045:     private static String joinBits(List<String> bits) {
2046:         if (bits == null || bits.isEmpty()) return "Dati tecnici da completare";
2047:         StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
2048:         for (String bit : bits) {
2049:             if (bit == null || bit.trim().isEmpty()) continue;
2050:             if (b.length() > 0) b.append("  •  ");
2051:             b.append(bit);
2052:         }
2053:         return b.length() == 0 ? "Dati tecnici da completare" : b.toString();
2054:     }

2056:     private LogEntry newEntryFromSession() {
2057:         SharedPreferences p = getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE);
2058:         long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
2059:         long cycle = p.getLong("lastCycleAt", 0L);
2060:         long printAt = p.getLong("lastPrintAt", 0L);
2061:         long testAt = p.getLong("lastTestAt", 0L);
2062:         long anchor = cycle > 0 ? cycle : Math.max(printAt, testAt);
2063:         if (anchor <= 0) anchor = now;
2064: 
2065:         LogEntry e = new LogEntry();
2066:         e.id = now;
2067:         e.timestamp = anchor;
2068:         long enlargementArmAt = p.getLong("pendingEnlargementAt", 0L);
2069:         e.enlargementMeta = (printAt > 0 && enlargementArmAt > 0 && enlargementArmAt <= printAt)
2070:                 ? p.getString("pendingEnlargementMeta", "") : "";
2071:         applyEnlargementSnapshotToVisibleLogFields(e);
2072:         // A print log always carries the latest completed print and the latest completed test strip.
2073:         // No time window: the user's rule is simply "associate the last test strip to the print".
2074:         if (printAt > 0 && printAt >= testAt) {
2075:             e.timestamp = printAt;
2076:             e.exposureMs = p.getInt("lastPrintMs", 0);
2077:             e.exposureMethod = TimingMath.normalizeMethod(p.getString("lastPrintMethod", TimingMath.METHOD_SECONDS));
2078:             e.exposureStep = p.getString("lastPrintStep", TimingMath.stepLabel(e.exposureMethod));
2079:             e.printSequence = p.getString("lastPrintSequence", "");
2080:             e.recipeState = p.getString("lastRecipeState", "");
2081:             if (testAt > 0) {
2082:                 e.testMs = p.getInt("lastTestMs", 0);
2083:                 e.testCount = p.getInt("lastTestCount", 0);
2084:                 e.testBaseFilterType = ExposureRecipe.normalizeFilter(p.getString("lastTestBaseFilterType", ExposureRecipe.FILTER_NONE));
2085:                 e.testBaseFilterValue = ExposureRecipe.snap5(p.getInt("lastTestBaseFilterValue", 0));
2086:                 e.testMethod = TimingMath.normalizeMethod(p.getString("lastTestMethod", TimingMath.METHOD_SECONDS));
2087:                 e.testStep = p.getString("lastTestStep", TimingMath.stepLabel(e.testMethod));
2088:                 e.testStripTimes = p.getString("lastTestStripTimes", "");
2089:                 e.testStripMethod = TimingMath.normalizeMaskingMethod(p.getString("lastTestStripMethod", TimingMath.MASK_REVEAL));
2090:             }
2091:         } else if (testAt > 0) {
2092:             // If the most recent completed cycle is only a test strip, keep the existing
2093:             // quick-save behaviour: test data only, until a final print is made.
2094:             e.timestamp = testAt;
2095:             e.testMs = p.getInt("lastTestMs", 0);
2096:             e.testCount = p.getInt("lastTestCount", 0);
2097:             e.testMethod = TimingMath.normalizeMethod(p.getString("lastTestMethod", TimingMath.METHOD_SECONDS));
2098:             e.testStep = p.getString("lastTestStep", TimingMath.stepLabel(e.testMethod));
2099:             e.testStripTimes = p.getString("lastTestStripTimes", "");
2100:             e.testStripMethod = TimingMath.normalizeMaskingMethod(p.getString("lastTestStripMethod", TimingMath.MASK_REVEAL));
2101:         }
2102: 
2103:         // Defaults for every new print card; all remain editable in the editor.
2104:         e.aperture = "11,5";
2105:         e.magenta = "0";
2106:         e.yellow = "0";
2107:         e.density = "0";
2108:         e.paper = "Fomaspeed Variant 311 RC lucida";
2109:         if (e.recipeState == null || e.recipeState.trim().isEmpty()) {
2110:             ExposureRecipe r = new ExposureRecipe();
2111:             if (e.exposureMs > 0) { r.originalBaseMs=e.exposureMs; r.operationalBaseMs=e.exposureMs; r.filterType=e.testBaseFilterType; r.filterValue=e.testBaseFilterValue; r.baseChosenAt=e.timestamp; e.recipeState=r.encode(); }
2112:         }
2113:         ExposureRecipe autoRecipe = ExposureRecipe.decode(e.recipeState);
2114:         if (autoRecipe.hasBase()) {
2115:             if (ExposureRecipe.FILTER_MAGENTA.equals(autoRecipe.filterType)) e.magenta=String.valueOf(autoRecipe.filterValue);
2116:             if (ExposureRecipe.FILTER_YELLOW.equals(autoRecipe.filterType)) e.yellow=String.valueOf(autoRecipe.filterValue);
2117:             e.density=autoRecipe.densityLabel();
2118:         }
2119:         applyReprintTemplate(e);
2120:         return e;
2121:     }

2123:     private boolean shouldOfferQuickSave() {
2124:         SharedPreferences session = getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE);
2125:         long cycle = session.getLong("lastCycleAt", 0L);
2126:         long saved = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).getLong("lastSavedCycleAt", 0L);
2127:         return cycle > saved;
2128:     }

2130:     private void markCurrentSessionSaved(LogEntry e) {
2131:         long cycle = getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE).getLong("lastCycleAt", 0L);
2132:         if (cycle > 0 && e.timestamp == cycle) {
2133:             getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putLong("lastSavedCycleAt", cycle).apply();
2134:         }
2135:         SharedPreferences template = getSharedPreferences("log_reprint", MODE_PRIVATE);
2136:         long activatedAt = template.getLong("activatedAt", Long.MAX_VALUE);
2137:         if (template.getBoolean("active", false) && e.exposureMs > 0 && e.timestamp >= activatedAt) {
2138:             template.edit().clear().apply();
2139:         }
2140:     }

2183:     private void applyReprintTemplate(LogEntry entry) {
2184:         if (entry == null || entry.exposureMs <= 0) return;
2185:         SharedPreferences template = getSharedPreferences("log_reprint", MODE_PRIVATE);
2186:         if (!template.getBoolean("active", false)) return;
2187:         long activatedAt = template.getLong("activatedAt", Long.MAX_VALUE);
2188:         if (entry.timestamp < activatedAt) return;
2189:         entry.title = template.getString("title", "");
2190:         entry.negative = template.getString("negative", "");
2191:         entry.aperture = template.getString("aperture", entry.aperture);
2192:         entry.columnHeight = template.getString("columnHeight", "");
2193:         entry.magenta = template.getString("magenta", entry.magenta);
2194:         entry.yellow = template.getString("yellow", entry.yellow);
2195:         entry.density = template.getString("density", entry.density);
2196:         entry.paper = template.getString("paper", entry.paper);
2197:         entry.notes = template.getString("notes", "");
2198:         entry.printSequence = template.getString("printSequence", "");
2199:         if (entry.recipeState == null || entry.recipeState.trim().isEmpty()) entry.recipeState = template.getString("recipeState", "");
2200:         if (entry.enlargementMeta == null || entry.enlargementMeta.trim().isEmpty()) entry.enlargementMeta = template.getString("enlargementMeta", "");
2201:         if (entry.testBaseFilterType == null || ExposureRecipe.FILTER_NONE.equals(entry.testBaseFilterType)) entry.testBaseFilterType = template.getString("testBaseFilterType", ExposureRecipe.FILTER_NONE);
2202:         if (entry.testBaseFilterValue <= 0) entry.testBaseFilterValue = template.getInt("testBaseFilterValue", 0);
2203:     }

2205:     private static String enlargementMetaValue(String meta, String key) {
2206:         if (meta == null || meta.trim().isEmpty()) return "";
2207:         for (String part : meta.split("\\|")) {
2208:             if (part.startsWith(key + "=")) return part.substring(key.length() + 1);
2209:         }
2210:         return "";
2211:     }

2213:     private static boolean applyEnlargementSnapshotToVisibleLogFields(LogEntry entry) {
2214:         if (entry == null || entry.enlargementMeta == null || entry.enlargementMeta.trim().isEmpty()) return false;
2215:         boolean changed = false;
2216:         String col = enlargementMetaValue(entry.enlargementMeta, "col");
2217:         if (!col.isEmpty()) {
2218:             try {
2219:                 String value = String.format(Locale.ITALY, "%.1f", Double.parseDouble(col));
2220:                 if (entry.columnHeight == null || !value.equals(entry.columnHeight.trim())) {
2221:                     entry.columnHeight = value;
2222:                     changed = true;
2223:                 }
2224:             } catch (Exception ignored) {}
2225:         }
2226:         String paper = enlargementMetaValue(entry.enlargementMeta, "paper");
2227:         if (!paper.isEmpty()) {
2228:             String format = paper.replace('.', ',').replace("x", " × ") + " cm";
2229:             String current = entry.paper == null ? "" : entry.paper.trim();
2230:             if (current.isEmpty()) {
2231:                 entry.paper = format;
2232:                 changed = true;
2233:             } else if (!current.contains(format) && !current.contains(paper)) {
2234:                 entry.paper = current + " · " + format;
2235:                 changed = true;
2236:             }
2393:             entry.favorite = favorite[0];
2394:             if (entry.id <= 0) entry.id = System.currentTimeMillis();
2395:             if (entry.timestamp <= 0) entry.timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();
2396:             LogStore.save(this, entry);
2397:             markCurrentSessionSaved(entry);
2398:             dialog.dismiss();
2399:             Toast.makeText(this, "Scheda salvata nel LOG", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
2400:             setMode(MODE_LOG);
2401:             refreshLogList();
2402:         });
2403:         panel.addView(save, lp(-1, dp(52)));

2419:                 resizePrint.setOnClickListener(v -> {
2420:                     entry.title = title.getText().toString().trim();
2421:                     entry.negative = negative[0];
2422:                     entry.aperture = aperture.getText().toString().trim();
2423:                     entry.columnHeight = column.getText().toString().trim();
2424:                     entry.magenta = magenta.getText().toString().trim();

1927:         }

1935:         if (e.magenta != null && !e.magenta.trim().isEmpty()) filterBits.add("M " + e.magenta.trim());
1936:         if (e.yellow != null && !e.yellow.trim().isEmpty()) filterBits.add("Y " + e.yellow.trim());

1948:                 if (item.exposureMs > 0) prints++;
1949:                 else if (item.testMs > 0) tests++;

1953:             if (prints > 0) bits.add(prints + (prints == 1 ? " stampa" : " stampe"));

1961:                 TextView plan = text("PIANO · " + recipe.summary(e.exposureMs) + "\n" + recipe.detail(e.exposureMs), 11, MUTED, false);
1962:                 plan.setPadding(0, dp(3), 0, 0);
1963:                 row.addView(plan, lp(-1, -2));
1964:             }

1966:             int[] strips = TimingMath.fromCsv(e.testStripTimes);
1967:             if (strips.length != e.testCount) strips = TimingMath.cumulativeSeries(e.testMethod, e.testMs, e.testCount);
1968:             String provino = "Provino · " + TimingMath.normalizeMethod(e.testMethod) + " · " + (e.testStep == null || e.testStep.trim().isEmpty() ? TimingMath.stepLabel(e.testMethod) : e.testStep) + " · " + TimingMath.normalizeMaskingMethod(e.testStripMethod) + "\nStrisce: " + TimingMath.seriesLabel(TimingMath.physicalTargets(strips, e.testStripMethod));

1971:             row.addView(test, lp(-1, -2));
