2044: 
2045:     private static String joinBits(List<String> bits) {
2046:         if (bits == null || bits.isEmpty()) return "Dati tecnici da completare";
2047:         StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
2048:         for (String bit : bits) {
2049:             if (bit == null || bit.trim().isEmpty()) continue;
2050:             if (b.length() > 0) b.append("  •  ");
2051:             b.append(bit);
2052:         }
2053:         return b.length() == 0 ? "Dati tecnici da completare" : b.toString();
2054:     }
2055: 
2056:     private LogEntry newEntryFromSession() {
2057:         SharedPreferences p = getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE);
2058:         long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
2059:         long cycle = p.getLong("lastCycleAt", 0L);
2060:         long printAt = p.getLong("lastPrintAt", 0L);
2061:         long testAt = p.getLong("lastTestAt", 0L);
2062:         long anchor = cycle > 0 ? cycle : Math.max(printAt, testAt);
2063:         if (anchor <= 0) anchor = now;
2064: 
2065:         LogEntry e = new LogEntry();
2066:         e.id = now;
2067:         e.timestamp = anchor;
2068:         long enlargementArmAt = p.getLong("pendingEnlargementAt", 0L);
2069:         e.enlargementMeta = (printAt > 0 && enlargementArmAt > 0 && enlargementArmAt <= printAt)
2070:                 ? p.getString("pendingEnlargementMeta", "") : "";
2071:         applyEnlargementSnapshotToVisibleLogFields(e);
2072:         // A print log always carries the latest completed print and the latest completed test strip.
2073:         // No time window: the user's rule is simply "associate the last test strip to the print".
2074:         if (printAt > 0 && printAt >= testAt) {
2075:             e.timestamp = printAt;
2076:             e.exposureMs = p.getInt("lastPrintMs", 0);
2077:             e.exposureMethod = TimingMath.normalizeMethod(p.getString("lastPrintMethod", TimingMath.METHOD_SECONDS));
2078:             e.exposureStep = p.getString("lastPrintStep", TimingMath.stepLabel(e.exposureMethod));
2079:             e.printSequence = p.getString("lastPrintSequence", "");
2080:             e.recipeState = p.getString("lastRecipeState", "");
2081:             if (testAt > 0) {
2082:                 e.testMs = p.getInt("lastTestMs", 0);
2083:                 e.testCount = p.getInt("lastTestCount", 0);
2084:                 e.testBaseFilterType = ExposureRecipe.normalizeFilter(p.getString("lastTestBaseFilterType", ExposureRecipe.FILTER_NONE));
2085:                 e.testBaseFilterValue = ExposureRecipe.snap5(p.getInt("lastTestBaseFilterValue", 0));
2086:                 e.testMethod = TimingMath.normalizeMethod(p.getString("lastTestMethod", TimingMath.METHOD_SECONDS));
2087:                 e.testStep = p.getString("lastTestStep", TimingMath.stepLabel(e.testMethod));
2088:                 e.testStripTimes = p.getString("lastTestStripTimes", "");
2089:                 e.testStripMethod = TimingMath.normalizeMaskingMethod(p.getString("lastTestStripMethod", TimingMath.MASK_REVEAL));
2090:             }
2091:         } else if (testAt > 0) {
2092:             // If the most recent completed cycle is only a test strip, keep the existing
2093:             // quick-save behaviour: test data only, until a final print is made.
2094:             e.timestamp = testAt;
2095:             e.testMs = p.getInt("lastTestMs", 0);
2096:             e.testCount = p.getInt("lastTestCount", 0);
2097:             e.testMethod = TimingMath.normalizeMethod(p.getString("lastTestMethod", TimingMath.METHOD_SECONDS));
2098:             e.testStep = p.getString("lastTestStep", TimingMath.stepLabel(e.testMethod));
2099:             e.testStripTimes = p.getString("lastTestStripTimes", "");
2100:             e.testStripMethod = TimingMath.normalizeMaskingMethod(p.getString("lastTestStripMethod", TimingMath.MASK_REVEAL));
2101:         }
2102: 
2103:         // Defaults for every new print card; all remain editable in the editor.
2104:         e.aperture = "11,5";
2105:         e.magenta = "0";
2106:         e.yellow = "0";
2107:         e.density = "0";
2108:         e.paper = "Fomaspeed Variant 311 RC lucida";
2109:         if (e.recipeState == null || e.recipeState.trim().isEmpty()) {
2110:             ExposureRecipe r = new ExposureRecipe();
2111:             if (e.exposureMs > 0) { r.originalBaseMs=e.exposureMs; r.operationalBaseMs=e.exposureMs; r.filterType=e.testBaseFilterType; r.filterValue=e.testBaseFilterValue; r.baseChosenAt=e.timestamp; e.recipeState=r.encode(); }
2112:         }
2113:         ExposureRecipe autoRecipe = ExposureRecipe.decode(e.recipeState);
2114:         if (autoRecipe.hasBase()) {
2115:             if (ExposureRecipe.FILTER_MAGENTA.equals(autoRecipe.filterType)) e.magenta=String.valueOf(autoRecipe.filterValue);
2116:             if (ExposureRecipe.FILTER_YELLOW.equals(autoRecipe.filterType)) e.yellow=String.valueOf(autoRecipe.filterValue);
2117:             e.density=autoRecipe.densityLabel();
2118:         }
2119:         applyReprintTemplate(e);
2246:         String col = enlargementMetaValue(meta, "col");
2247:         String fill = enlargementMetaValue(meta, "fill");
2248:         String mode = "0".equals(fill) ? "immagine intera" : ("1".equals(fill) ? "riempi larghezza" : ("2".equals(fill) ? "riempi altezza" : ""));
2249:         StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
2250:         if (!paper.isEmpty()) b.append("formato carta ").append(paper).append(" cm · orizzontale");
2251:         if (!lens.isEmpty()) b.append(b.length() > 0 ? " · " : "").append(lens).append(" mm");
2252:         if (!beta.isEmpty()) {
2253:             try { b.append(b.length() > 0 ? " · " : "").append("β ").append(String.format(Locale.ITALY, "%.3f", Double.parseDouble(beta))); }
2254:             catch (Exception ignored) {}
2255:         }
2256:         if (!col.isEmpty()) {
2257:             try { b.append(b.length() > 0 ? " · " : "").append("colonna ").append(String.format(Locale.ITALY, "%.1f", Double.parseDouble(col))); }
2258:             catch (Exception ignored) {}
2259:         }
2260:         if (!mode.isEmpty()) b.append(b.length() > 0 ? " · " : "").append(mode);
2261:         return b.length() == 0 ? "—" : b.toString();
2262:     }
2263: 
2264:     private void showLogEditor(final LogEntry entry, final boolean isNew) {
2265:         if (applyEnlargementSnapshotToVisibleLogFields(entry) && !isNew) LogStore.save(this, entry);
2266:         if (darkroomMode) {
2267:             showAppConfirmDialog("COMPILAZIONE SCHEDA",
2268:                     "Per scrivere nei campi manuali è meglio uscire dalla modalità camera oscura: la tastiera di Android non è inattinica. Puoi comunque consultare il LOG in rosso.",
2269:                     "VAI ALLE IMPOSTAZIONI", this::showSettingsDialog, "ANNULLA");
2270:             return;
2271:         }
2272:

855:     private void showSplitGradeEditor(final boolean creating) {
856:         if (darkroomMode) return;
857:         if (printSequence == null) printSequence = new PrintSequence();
858:         final SplitGradePlan original = printSequence.split == null ? new SplitGradePlan() : printSequence.split.copy();
859:         final SplitGradePlan draft = original.copy();
860:         draft.enabled = true;
861:         if (creating) {
862:             if (printWidthMs < 1000) {
863:                 Toast.makeText(this, "Per lo Split Grade la base deve essere almeno 1,0 s", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
864:                 return;
865:             }
866:             // Default operativo di riferimento: Fomaspeed Variant 311 RC + testa colore Meopta.
867:             draft.softYellow = 60;
868:             draft.hardMagenta = 180;
869:             int units = Math.max(2, printWidthMs / 500);
870:             int softUnits = (units + 1) / 2;
871:             draft.softMs = softUnits * 500;
872:             draft.hardMs = (units - softUnits) * 500;
873:         }
874:         draft.sanitize();
875:         if (draft.totalMs() > printWidthMs && printWidthMs >= 1000) {
876:             int units = Math.max(2, printWidthMs / 500);
877:             int total = Math.max(1, draft.totalMs());
878:             int softUnits = Math.max(1, Math.min(units - 1, Math.round((draft.softMs / (float) total) * units)));
879:             draft.softMs = softUnits * 500;
880:             draft.hardMs = (units - softUnits) * 500;
881:         }
882: 
883:         final Dialog dialog = new Dialog(this);
884:         dialog.requestWindowFeature(Window.FEATURE_NO_TITLE);
885:         ScrollView sc = new ScrollView(this);
886:         LinearLayout panel = new LinearLayout(this);
887:         panel.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
888:         panel.setPadding(dp(18), dp(16), dp(18), dp(18));
889:         panel.setBackground(roundRect(CARD, 14, 1, BORDER));
890:         sc.addView(panel, new ScrollView.LayoutParams(-1, -2));
891: 
892:         panel.addView(text("SPLIT GRADE", 20, SPLIT_VIVA_MAGENTA, true), lp(-1, -2));
893:         TextView note = text("Foma Variant + Meopta · MORBIDA 60Y / 0M · DURA 0Y / 180M · valori modificabili", 12, MUTED, false);
894:         note.setPadding(0, dp(4), 0, dp(6));
895:         panel.addView(note, lp(-1, -2));
896:         Button splitInfo = compactButton("ⓘ  COME FUNZIONA");
897:         splitInfo.setTextSize(11);
898:         splitInfo.setOnClickListener(v -> showAppConfirmDialog(
899:                 "COME FUNZIONA LO SPLIT GRADE",
900:                 "MORBIDO / GIALLO: agisce soprattutto sulla resa dei toni chiari e produce una risposta a contrasto più basso.\n\n"
901:                         + "DURO / MAGENTA: aumenta soprattutto la separazione delle ombre e la profondità dei neri.\n\n"
902:                         + "Le due esposizioni NON sono indipendenti: modificare la dura può influire anche sui toni chiari e viceversa. Non significa quindi ‘giallo = solo luci’ o ‘magenta = solo ombre’.",
903:                 null, null, "CHIUDI"));
904:         panel.addView(splitInfo, margin(lp(-1, dp(42)), 0, 0, 0, 10));

1046:             fm.setOnClickListener(v->{burnMode[0]=PrintCorrection.BURN_FILTER_M_SPLIT;phase[0]=PrintCorrection.PHASE_HARD;filterStyle[0].run();});
1047:             fc.setOnClickListener(v->{

1049:                     burnMode[0]=PrintCorrection.BURN_FILTER_CUSTOM_Y;
1050:                 phase[0]=PrintCorrection.BURN_FILTER_CUSTOM_M.equals(burnMode[0])?PrintCorrection.PHASE_HARD:PrintCorrection.PHASE_SOFT;
1051:                 filterStyle[0].run();

1056:             panel.addView(filters,margin(lp(-1,-2),0,0,0,7));

1122:             c.label=name.isEmpty()?(c.isDodge()?"Zona da mascherare":"Zona da bruciare"):name;

1125:                 int baseMs=(printSequence.hasSplit()&&c.isBoth())?Math.min(printSequence.split.softMs,printSequence.split.hardMs):printSequence.baseMsFor(c,printWidthMs);

1160:     private boolean validatePrintSequenceForBase() {
1161:         if (printSequence == null || printSequence.isEmpty()) return true;
1162:         if (printSequence.hasSplit()) {
1163:             // I due tempi Split Grade sono esposizioni sperimentali distinte.
1164:             // Non esiste alcun vincolo rispetto al vecchio tempo singolo.
1165:             printSequence.split.sanitize();
1166:         }
1167:         for (PrintCorrection c : printSequence.dodges()) {
1168:             if (printSequence.hasSplit() && c.isBoth()) {
1169:                 int softCue = c.resolvedMs(printSequence.split.softMs);
1170:                 int hardCue = c.resolvedMs(printSequence.split.hardMs);
1171:                 if (softCue >= printSequence.split.softMs || hardCue >= printSequence.split.hardMs) {
1172:                     setStatusPresentation("ATTENZIONE", "DODGE " + c.safeLabel() + ": deve poter terminare prima della fine sia del giallo sia del magenta", RED);
1173:                     return false;
1174:                 }
1175:                 continue;
1176:             }
1177:             int baseMs = printSequence.baseMsFor(c, printWidthMs);
1178:             int cueMs = c.resolvedMs(baseMs);
1179:             if (cueMs >= baseMs) {
1180:                 setStatusPresentation("ATTENZIONE", "DODGE " + c.safeLabel() + ": il cue deve avvenire prima della fine della esposizione " + (printSequence.hasSplit() ? (c.isHard() ? "magenta" : "gialla") : "base"), RED);
1181:                 return false;
1182:             }
1183:         }
1184:         return true;
1185:     }

1197:         getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
1198:                 .putString("testBaseFilterType", ExposureRecipe.normalizeFilter(testBaseFilterType))
1199:                 .putInt("testBaseFilterValue", ExposureRecipe.snap5(testBaseFilterValue))
1200:                 .apply();
1201:         if (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_SOFT) {
1202:             splitSoftYellow = ExposureRecipe.snap5(testBaseFilterValue);
1203:             invalidateSplitHardChoice();
1204:             persistSplitProvinoState();
1205:         } else if (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_HARD) {
1206:             splitHardMagenta = ExposureRecipe.snap5(testBaseFilterValue);
1207:             persistSplitProvinoState();
1208:         }
1209:         refreshTestBaseFilterUi();
1210:         refreshSplitProvinoUi();
1211:     }

1315:     private void showLengthenTimesDialog() {
1316:         if (darkroomMode || armed || !canLengthenTimes()) return;
1317:         ensureExposureRecipeBase();
1318:         final int currentQ = ExposureRecipe.clampDensity(exposureRecipe.densityQuarterSteps);
1319:         final int[] targetQ = {currentQ};
1320:         final Dialog dialog = new Dialog(this); dialog.requestWindowFeature(Window.FEATURE_NO_TITLE);
1321:         LinearLayout panel = new LinearLayout(this); panel.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL); panel.setPadding(dp(18),dp(16),dp(18),dp(18)); panel.setBackground(roundRect(CARD,14,1,BORDER));

3023:     private void createSplitPrintFromProvino() {
3024:         if (splitSoftChosenMs <= 0 || splitHardChosenMs <= 0) return;
3025:         SplitGradePlan plan=new SplitGradePlan();
3026:         plan.enabled=true;
3027:         plan.softYellow=ExposureRecipe.snap5(splitSoftYellow);
3028:         plan.softMs=snap(splitSoftChosenMs,500,36_000_000);
3029:         plan.hardMagenta=ExposureRecipe.snap5(splitHardMagenta);
3030:         plan.hardMs=snap(splitHardChosenMs,500,36_000_000);
3031:         plan.sanitize();
3032:         PrintSequence next=new PrintSequence();
3033:         next.split=plan;
3034:         // New experimentally determined base: do not silently inherit old Dodge/Burn.
3035:         printSequence=next;
3036:         getSharedPreferences("ui",MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putString("printSequence",printSequence.encode()).apply();
3037:         persistPrintSequence();
3038: 
3039:         int softMs=plan.softMs, hardMs=plan.hardMs, sy=plan.softYellow, hm=plan.hardMagenta;
3040:         provinoFlow=PROVINO_SINGLE;
3041:         testBaseFilterType=ExposureRecipe.normalizeFilter(splitReturnFilterType);
3042:         testBaseFilterValue=ExposureRecipe.snap5(splitReturnFilterValue);
3043:         testWidthMs=snap(splitReturnTestWidthMs,500,30_000);
3044:         persistSplitProvinoState();
3045:         refreshTestBaseFilterUi();
3046:         setMode(MODE_PRINT);
3047:         updatePrintSequenceUi();
3048:         setStatusPresentation("SPLIT GRADE DAL PROVINO",
3049:                 "MORBIDO · "+sy+"Y / 0M · "+formatTime(softMs)+"  +  DURO · 0Y / "+hm+"M · "+formatTime(hardMs)+". Due esposizioni consecutive, tempi indipendenti.", GREEN);
3050:     }

3052:     private void disarm() {
3053:         if (device == null || !device.isValid()) {
3054:             stateText.setText("Nessun SONOFF DIY verificato");
3055:             return;
3056:         }
3057:         stateText.setTextColor(MUTED);
3058:         stateText.setText("Ripristino ON/OFF normale…");
3059:         Intent i = new Intent(this, SonoffArmService.class).setAction(SonoffArmService.ACTION_DISARM);
3060:         startServiceCompat(i);
3061:     }
3062: 
3063:     private void startServiceCompat(Intent intent) {
3064:         if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) {
3065:             try {
3066:                 java.lang.reflect.Method m = Context.class.getMethod("startForegroundService", Intent.class);
3067:                 m.invoke(this, intent);
3068:                 return;
3069:             } catch (Exception ignored) {}
3070:         }
3071:         startService(intent);
3072:     }

3075:         if (armed) return;

3077:         else setPrintTime(printWidthMs + direction * 500);
3078:     }

3081:         if (armed) return;

3083:         else setTestTime(testWidthMs + direction * 500);

3087:         if (armed) return;

3091:         SharedPreferences.Editor edit = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putInt("printWidthMs", printWidthMs);
3092:         if (exposureRecipe.hasBase()) edit.putString("exposureRecipe", exposureRecipe.encode());
3093:         edit.apply();
3094:         printTimeText.setText(formatTime(printWidthMs));
3095:         updatePrintSequenceUi();
3096:         applyModeUi();
3097:     }

3124:     private void updateSelectionUiBeforeDiscovery() {
3125:         if (selectedDeviceId == null || selectedDeviceId.isEmpty()) {
3126:             deviceStatus.setText("●  SONOFF NON CONFIGURATO");
3127:             deviceStatus.setTextColor(MUTED);
3128:             selectDeviceButton.setText("⚙");
3129:             stateText.setText("Apri le impostazioni e scegli il SONOFF dell’ingranditore.");
3130:         } else {
3131:             deviceStatus.setText("●  SONOFF — connessione…");

===== applyModeUi =====
276:         splitReturnFilterValue = ExposureRecipe.snap5(p.getInt("splitProvinoReturnFilterValue", testBaseFilterValue));
277:         splitReturnTestWidthMs = p.getInt("splitProvinoReturnTestWidthMs", testWidthMs);
278:         if (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_SOFT) {
279:             testBaseFilterType = ExposureRecipe.FILTER_YELLOW;
280:             testBaseFilterValue = splitSoftYellow;
281:         } else if (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_HARD) {
282:             if (splitSoftChosenMs <= 0) provinoFlow = PROVINO_SPLIT_SOFT;
283:             testBaseFilterType = provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_HARD ? ExposureRecipe.FILTER_MAGENTA : ExposureRecipe.FILTER_YELLOW;
284:             testBaseFilterValue = provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_HARD ? splitHardMagenta : splitSoftYellow;
285:         }
286: 
287:         DeviceConfig saved = DeviceConfig.load(this);
288:         selectedDeviceId = saved.deviceId == null ? "" : saved.deviceId.trim();
289:         device = null;
290:         DeviceConfig savedSafelight = SafelightConfig.load(this);
291:         selectedSafelightDeviceId = savedSafelight.deviceId == null ? "" : savedSafelight.deviceId.trim();
292:         safelightDevice = savedSafelight.isValid() ? savedSafelight : null;
293:         buildUi();
294:         applyModeUi();
295:         updateSelectionUiBeforeDiscovery();
296:     }

298:     @Override protected void onStart() {
299:         super.onStart();
300:         activityStarted = true;
301:         IntentFilter f = new IntentFilter(SonoffArmService.BROADCAST_STATE);
302:         if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) {
303:             try {
304:                 java.lang.reflect.Method m = Context.class.getMethod("registerReceiver", BroadcastReceiver.class, IntentFilter.class, int.class);
305:                 m.invoke(this, stateReceiver, f, 4); // RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED
306:             } catch (Exception e) {
307:                 registerReceiver(stateReceiver, f);
308:             }
309:         } else {
310:             registerReceiver(stateReceiver, f);
311:         }
312:         restoreRuntimeState();
313:         foundDevices.clear();
314:         discovery = new SonoffDiscovery(this, this);
315:         discovery.start();
316: 
317:         // Do not depend only on mDNS. The selected MINIR2 was already verified in
318:         // previous runs, so probe the last known address immediately while NSD
319:         // independently searches by immutable Device ID (and can update the IP).
320:         DeviceConfig saved = DeviceConfig.load(this);
321:         if (saved.isValid() && saved.deviceId.equals(selectedDeviceId)) {
322:             validateSelected(saved);
323:         }
324:         reconnectHandler.removeCallbacks(reconnectRunnable);
325:         reconnectHandler.postDelayed(reconnectRunnable, 3000L);
326:         ensureSafelightIdleOn();
327:     }
328: 
329:     @Override protected void onResume() {
330:         super.onResume();
331:         SharedPreferences ep = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE);

386:     private void applyRuntimeState(String state, String message) {
387:         if (state == null || state.trim().isEmpty()) state = SonoffArmService.STATE_NORMAL;
388: 
389:         boolean blankNormalRefresh = SonoffArmService.STATE_NORMAL.equals(state)
390:                 && (message == null || message.trim().isEmpty());
391:         if (blankNormalRefresh && android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis() < transientCompletionUntilMs) {
392:             return;
393:         }
394: 
395:         statusResetHandler.removeCallbacks(readyResetRunnable);
396:         if (!SonoffArmService.STATE_NORMAL.equals(state)) transientCompletionUntilMs = 0L;
397: 
398:         boolean cancellable = SonoffArmService.STATE_ARMED.equals(state)
399:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_ARMING.equals(state)
400:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_EXPOSING.equals(state)
401:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_PAUSING.equals(state)
402:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_WAITING_BURN.equals(state)
403:                 || SonoffArmService.STATE_WAITING_SPLIT.equals(state);
404:         boolean busy = cancellable || SonoffArmService.STATE_DISARMING.equals(state);
405: 
406:         if (busy) {
407:             armed = true;
408:             setControlsEnabled(false);
409:             normalButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);
410:         } else if (SonoffArmService.STATE_ERROR.equals(state)) {
411:             armed = false;
412:             setControlsEnabled(device != null && device.isValid());
413:             normalButton.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
414:             normalButton.setEnabled(device != null && device.isValid());
415:             normalButton.setAlpha(normalButton.isEnabled() ? 1f : (darkroomMode ? 0.62f : 0.45f));
416:             actionButton.setVisibility(mode == MODE_LOG ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
417:             actionButton.setText("RIPROVA");
418:             actionButton.setEnabled(device != null && device.isValid());
419:             actionButton.setAlpha(actionButton.isEnabled() ? 1f : (darkroomMode ? 0.62f : 0.45f));
420:             setStatusPresentation("ATTENZIONE", message == null ? "Errore del ciclo" : message, RED);
421:             cancelCycleButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);
422:             // An exposure error must not disable the independent manual ON/OFF safelight interlock.
423:             ensureSafelightIdleOn();
424:             return;
425:         } else {
426:             armed = false;
427:             normalButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);
428:             setControlsEnabled(device != null && device.isValid());
429:         }
430: 
431:         cancelCycleButton.setVisibility(cancellable ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
432:         if (!SonoffArmService.STATE_DISARMING.equals(state)) cancelCycleButton.setEnabled(true);
433:         actionButton.setVisibility(mode == MODE_LOG || cancellable || SonoffArmService.STATE_DISARMING.equals(state) ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
434: 
435:         String detail = message == null ? "" : message.trim();
436:         int accent = MUTED;
437:         String title = "PRONTO";
438:         boolean transientCompletion = false;
439:         if (SonoffArmService.STATE_ARMING.equals(state)) {
753:         if (!printSequence.corrections.isEmpty()) {
754:             s.append("\n\nCORREZIONI");
755:             for (PrintCorrection c : printSequence.corrections) {
756:                 if (c == null) continue;
757:                 int baseMs = printSequence.baseMsFor(c, printWidthMs);
758:                 s.append('\n').append(c.displayLine(baseMs, printSequence.hasSplit()));
759:             }
760:         }
761:         if (exposureRecipe != null && exposureRecipe.globalQuarterStops != 0)
762:             s.append("\n\nCORREZIONE GLOBALE · ").append(exposureRecipe.globalLabel());
763:         printSequenceSummary.setText(s.toString());
764:         printSequenceSummary.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
765:     }

770:         updatePrintSequenceUi();
771:         applyModeUi();
772:     }

855:     private void showSplitGradeEditor(final boolean creating) {
856:         if (darkroomMode) return;
857:         if (printSequence == null) printSequence = new PrintSequence();
858:         final SplitGradePlan original = printSequence.split == null ? new SplitGradePlan() : printSequence.split.copy();
859:         final SplitGradePlan draft = original.copy();
860:         draft.enabled = true;
861:         if (creating) {
862:             if (printWidthMs < 1000) {
863:                 Toast.makeText(this, "Per lo Split Grade la base deve essere almeno 1,0 s", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
864:                 return;
865:             }
1282:             if (printSequence.hasSplit()) {
1283:                 printSequence.split.softMs = ExposureRecipe.scaledMs(printSequence.split.softMs, quarterStopDelta);
1284:                 printSequence.split.hardMs = ExposureRecipe.scaledMs(printSequence.split.hardMs, quarterStopDelta);
1285:                 printSequence.split.sanitize();
1286:             }
1287:             for (PrintCorrection c : printSequence.corrections) {
1288:                 if (c == null || c.quarterStops > 0) continue;
1289:                 c.milliseconds = ExposureRecipe.scaledMs(c.milliseconds, quarterStopDelta);
1290:             }
1291:         }
1292:         exposureRecipe.operationalBaseMs = newBase;
1293:         printWidthMs = newBase;
1294:         getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
1295:                 .putInt("printWidthMs", printWidthMs)
1296:                 .putString("printSequence", printSequence == null ? "" : printSequence.encode())
1297:                 .putString("exposureRecipe", exposureRecipe.encode()).apply();
1298:         if (printTimeText != null) printTimeText.setText(formatTime(printWidthMs));
1299:         updatePrintSequenceUi();
1300:         applyModeUi();
1301:     }
1302: 
1303:     private String recipeBaseSummary() {
1304:         if (exposureRecipe == null || !exposureRecipe.hasBase()) return "";
1305:         if (printSequence != null && printSequence.hasSplit()) {
1306:             return "BASE DI PARTENZA · " + exposureRecipe.originalLine()
1307:                     + (exposureRecipe.densityQuarterSteps > 0 ? "\nDENSITÀ OPERATIVA · " + exposureRecipe.densityLabel() + " · applicata alla ricetta finale" : "");
1308:         }
1309:         String s = "BASE · " + exposureRecipe.operationalLine(printWidthMs);
1310:         if (exposureRecipe.originalBaseMs > 0 && (exposureRecipe.originalBaseMs != exposureRecipe.operationalBaseMs || exposureRecipe.densityQuarterSteps > 0))
1311:             s = "BASE ORIGINALE · " + exposureRecipe.originalLine() + "\nBASE OPERATIVA · " + exposureRecipe.operationalLine(printWidthMs);
1312:         return s;
1313:     }

1370:     private String recipeOriginalLabel(LogEntry entry, String fallback) {
1371:         ExposureRecipe r=ExposureRecipe.decode(entry==null?"":entry.recipeState);
1372:         return r.hasBase()?r.originalLine():fallback;
1373:     }
1374:     private String recipeOperationalLabel(LogEntry entry, String fallback) {
1375:         ExposureRecipe r=ExposureRecipe.decode(entry==null?"":entry.recipeState);
1376:         return r.hasBase()?r.operationalLine(entry.exposureMs):fallback;
1377:     }

2759:         stateCard.setVisibility(log ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
2760:         cancelCycleButton.setVisibility(armed ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);

2762:

2777:     private void arm() {
2778:         if (mode == MODE_LOG) return;
2779:         if (device == null || !device.isValid()) {
2780:             stateText.setText("Il SONOFF dell’ingranditore non è ancora verificato in DIY");
2781:             return;
2782:         }
2783:         if (mode == MODE_PRINT && !validatePrintSequenceForBase()) return;
2784:         if (safelightAuto) {
2785:             DeviceConfig safe = SafelightConfig.load(this);
2786:             if (!safe.isValid() || safe.deviceId.equals(device.deviceId)) {
2787:                 setStatusPresentation("ATTENZIONE", "Configura un secondo SONOFF DIY dedicato alla luce rossa", RED);
2788:                 return;
2789:             }
2790:         }
2791:         armed = true;
2792:         cancelCycleButton.setEnabled(true);
2793:         setStatusPresentation("PREPARAZIONE", mode == MODE_PRINT ? "Imposto Inching…" : "Preparo il provino…", AMBER);
2794:         setControlsEnabled(false);
2795: 
2796:         Intent i;
2797:         if (mode == MODE_PRINT) {
2798:             SharedPreferences activeUi = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE);
2799:             getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
2800:                     .putString("pendingEnlargementMeta", activeUi.getString("enlargementMeta", ""))
2801:                     .putLong("pendingEnlargementAt", System.currentTimeMillis())
2802:                     .apply();
2803:             i = new Intent(this, SonoffArmService.class).setAction(SonoffArmService.ACTION_ARM_PRINT);
2804:             ensureExposureRecipeBase();
2805:             persistExposureRecipe();
2806:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_WIDTH, printWidthMs);
2807:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TIMING_METHOD, timingMethod);
2808:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_PRINT_SEQUENCE, printSequence == null ? "" : printSequence.encode());
2809:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_RECIPE_STATE, exposureRecipe.encode());
2810:         } else {
2811:             i = new Intent(this, SonoffArmService.class).setAction(SonoffArmService.ACTION_ARM_TEST);
2812:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_WIDTH, testWidthMs);
2813:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_COUNT, testCount);
2814:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_PAUSE, testPauseMs);
2815:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TIMING_METHOD, timingMethod);
2816:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_TARGETS, currentTestStripTargets());
2817:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_MASKING_METHOD, TimingMath.normalizeMaskingMethod(testStripMethod));
2818:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_FILTER_TYPE, ExposureRecipe.normalizeFilter(testBaseFilterType));
2819:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_FILTER_VALUE, ExposureRecipe.snap5(testBaseFilterValue));
2820:             if (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_HARD && splitSoftChosenMs > 0) {
2821:                 i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_PRE_EXPOSURE_MS, snap(splitSoftChosenMs, 500, 36_000_000));
2822:                 i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_PRE_EXPOSURE_FILTER_TYPE, ExposureRecipe.FILTER_YELLOW);
2823:                 i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_PRE_EXPOSURE_FILTER_VALUE, ExposureRecipe.snap5(splitSoftYellow));
2824:             }
2825:         }
2826:         startServiceCompat(i);
2827:     }

3087:         if (armed) return;

3089:         if (exposureRecipe == null) exposureRecipe = new ExposureRecipe();
3090:         if (exposureRecipe.hasBase()) exposureRecipe.operationalBaseMs = printWidthMs;
3091:         SharedPreferences.Editor edit = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putInt("printWidthMs", printWidthMs);
3092:         if (exposureRecipe.hasBase()) edit.putString("exposureRecipe", exposureRecipe.encode());
3093:         edit.apply();

3095:         updatePrintSequenceUi();

3138:     private void updateSafelightStatus() {
3139:         if (safelightStatus == null) return;
3140:         DeviceConfig safe = SafelightConfig.load(this);
3141:         if (!safelightAuto) {
3142:             safelightStatus.setText("LUCE ROSSA AUTOMATICA — OFF");
3143:             safelightStatus.setTextColor(MUTED);
3144:         } else if (!safe.isValid()) {
3145:             safelightStatus.setText("LUCE ROSSA AUTOMATICA — DA CONFIGURARE");
3146:             safelightStatus.setTextColor(RED);
3147:         } else {
3148:             safelightStatus.setText("LUCE ROSSA AUTOMATICA — ON");
3149:             safelightStatus.setTextColor(darkroomMode ? RED : Color.rgb(201, 157, 70));
3150:         }
3151:     }
3152: 
3153:     private void showSafelightPicker() {
3154:         ArrayList<FoundDevice> list = new ArrayList<>();
3155:         for (FoundDevice f : foundDevices.values()) {
3156:             if (!f.diyCandidate) continue;
3157:             if (f.config.deviceId.equals(selectedDeviceId)) continue;
3158:             list.add(f);
3159:         }
3160:         Collections.sort(list, Comparator.comparing(a -> a.config.deviceId));
3161:         if (list.isEmpty()) {
3162:             showAppConfirmDialog("NESSUN SECONDO SONOFF DIY TROVATO",
3163:                     "La luce rossa richiede un secondo SONOFF in modalità DIY, diverso da quello dell'ingranditore. Attendi la ricerca di rete e riprova.",
3164:                     "OK", null, null);
3165:             return;
3166:         }
3167:         String[] labels = new String[list.size()];
3168:         for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {
3169:             FoundDevice f = list.get(i);
3170:             String selected = f.config.deviceId.equals(selectedSafelightDeviceId) ? "  ✓" : "";
3171:             labels[i] = "ID " + f.config.deviceId + selected + "\n" + f.config.host + ":" + f.config.port + " • DIY";
3172:         }
3173:         showAppChoiceDialog("SCEGLI IL SONOFF DELLA LUCE ROSSA", labels,
3174:                 which -> selectSafelight(list.get(which)), "ANNULLA");
3175:     }
3176: 
3177:     private void selectSafelight(FoundDevice found) {
3178:         if (found == null || !found.diyCandidate || !found.config.isValid()) return;
3179:         if (found.config.deviceId.equals(selectedDeviceId)) {
3180:             Toast.makeText(this, "Scegli un SONOFF diverso da quello dell’ingranditore", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
3181:             return;
3182:         }
3183:         selectedSafelightDeviceId = found.config.deviceId;
3184:         safelightDevice = found.config;
3185:         SafelightConfig.save(this, found.config);
3186:         updateSafelightStatus();
3187:         if (safelightAuto) ensureSafelightIdleOn();

3153:     private void showSafelightPicker() {
3154:         ArrayList<FoundDevice> list = new ArrayList<>();
3155:         for (FoundDevice f : foundDevices.values()) {
3156:             if (!f.diyCandidate) continue;
3157:             if (f.config.deviceId.equals(selectedDeviceId)) continue;
3158:             list.add(f);
3159:         }
3160:         Collections.sort(list, Comparator.comparing(a -> a.config.deviceId));
3161:         if (list.isEmpty()) {
3162:             showAppConfirmDialog("NESSUN SECONDO SONOFF DIY TROVATO",
3163:                     "La luce rossa richiede un secondo SONOFF in modalità DIY, diverso da quello dell'ingranditore. Attendi la ricerca di rete e riprova.",
3164:                     "OK", null, null);
3165:             return;
3166:         }
3167:         String[] labels = new String[list.size()];
3168:         for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {
3169:             FoundDevice f = list.get(i);
3170:             String selected = f.config.deviceId.equals(selectedSafelightDeviceId) ? "  ✓" : "";
3171:             labels[i] = "ID " + f.config.deviceId + selected + "\n" + f.config.host + ":" + f.config.port + " • DIY";
3172:         }

3174:                 which -> selectSafelight(list.get(which)), "ANNULLA");
3175:     }

3177:     private void selectSafelight(FoundDevice found) {
3178:         if (found == null || !found.diyCandidate || !found.config.isValid()) return;
3179:         if (found.config.deviceId.equals(selectedDeviceId)) {
3180:             Toast.makeText(this, "Scegli un SONOFF diverso da quello dell’ingranditore", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();

3182:         }

3184:         safelightDevice = found.config;
3185:         SafelightConfig.save(this, found.config);
3186:         updateSafelightStatus();
3187:         if (safelightAuto) ensureSafelightIdleOn();
3188:         Toast.makeText(this, "SONOFF luce rossa selezionato", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
3189:     }

3698:     private TextView fStopBadge(boolean compact) {
3699:         int accent = darkroomMode ? RED : Color.rgb(201, 157, 70);
3700:         int fill = darkroomMode ? Color.BLACK : Color.rgb(31, 29, 24);
3701:         TextView badge = text(compact ? "F-STOP  ·  ¼" : "F-STOP  ·  ¼ stop", compact ? 10 : 12, accent, true);
3702:         badge.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
3703:         badge.setPadding(dp(compact ? 8 : 12), dp(compact ? 3 : 4), dp(compact ? 8 : 12), dp(compact ? 3 : 4));
3704:         badge.setBackground(roundRect(fill, compact ? 10 : 13, 1, accent));
3705:         badge.setContentDescription("Modalità F-STOP, passo un quarto di stop");
3706:         return badge;
3707:     }

3713:         parent.addView(badge, margin(lp(compact ? -2 : -1, dp(compact ? 26 : 32)), compact ? 0 : dp(36), dp(6), compact ? 0 : dp(36), dp(6)));
3714:         return badge;
3715:     }

724:     private void updatePrintSequenceUi() {
725:         if (printSequenceButton == null || printSequenceSummary == null) return;
726:         if (printSequence == null) printSequence = new PrintSequence();
727:         printSequenceButton.setText("PIANO DI STAMPA");
728: 
729:         String base = recipeBaseSummary();
730:         boolean hasRecipe = !base.isEmpty() || !printSequence.isEmpty()
731:                 || (exposureRecipe != null && exposureRecipe.globalQuarterStops != 0);
732:         if (!hasRecipe) {
733:             printSequenceSummary.setText("");
734:             printSequenceSummary.setVisibility(View.GONE);
735:             return;
736:         }
737: 
738:         StringBuilder s = new StringBuilder();
739:         if (!base.isEmpty()) s.append(base);
740:         if (s.length() > 0) s.append("\n\n");
741:         s.append("ESPOSIZIONE\n");
742:         if (printSequence.hasSplit()) {
743:             s.append(printSequence.split.softLine()).append('\n').append(printSequence.split.hardLine());
744:         } else {
745:             s.append("SINGOLA · ").append(formatTime(printWidthMs));
746:             if (exposureRecipe != null && exposureRecipe.hasBase()) {
747:                 String f = exposureRecipe.filterLabel();
748:                 if (!"NESSUNO".equals(f)) s.append(" · ").append(f);
2759:         stateCard.setVisibility(log ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
2760:         cancelCycleButton.setVisibility(armed ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
2761:         saveLogButton.setVisibility(!log && !armed && shouldOfferQuickSave() ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
2762: 
2763:         styleNavButton(printModeButton, print, GREEN);
2764:         styleNavButton(testModeButton, test, BLUE);
2765:         styleNavButton(logModeButton, log, LOG_ACCENT);
2766:         actionButton.setTextColor(darkroomMode ? Color.BLACK : TEXT_PRIMARY);
2767:         if (!log) {
2768:             actionButton.setBackground(roundRect(print ? GREEN : BLUE, 10, 0, 0));
2769:             actionButton.setText(print ? (printSequence != null && !printSequence.isEmpty() ? "ARMA PIANO DI STAMPA" : "ARMA STAMPA • " + formatTime(printWidthMs))
2770:                     : (TimingMath.isFStop(timingMethod)
2771:                         ? "ARMA PROVINO • " + testCount + " STRISCE • ¼ stop"
2772:                         : "ARMA PROVINO • " + testCount + " × " + formatTime(testWidthMs)));
2773:         }
2774:         refreshSplitProvinoUi();
2775:     }

2777:     private void arm() {
2778:         if (mode == MODE_LOG) return;
2779:         if (device == null || !device.isValid()) {
2780:             stateText.setText("Il SONOFF dell’ingranditore non è ancora verificato in DIY");
2781:             return;
2782:         }
2783:         if (mode == MODE_PRINT && !validatePrintSequenceForBase()) return;
2784:         if (safelightAuto) {
2785:             DeviceConfig safe = SafelightConfig.load(this);
2786:             if (!safe.isValid() || safe.deviceId.equals(device.deviceId)) {
2787:                 setStatusPresentation("ATTENZIONE", "Configura un secondo SONOFF DIY dedicato alla luce rossa", RED);
2788:                 return;
2789:             }
2790:         }
2791:         armed = true;
2792:         cancelCycleButton.setEnabled(true);
2793:         setStatusPresentation("PREPARAZIONE", mode == MODE_PRINT ? "Imposto Inching…" : "Preparo il provino…", AMBER);
2794:         setControlsEnabled(false);
2795: 
2796:         Intent i;
2797:         if (mode == MODE_PRINT) {
2798:             SharedPreferences activeUi = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE);
2799:             getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
2800:                     .putString("pendingEnlargementMeta", activeUi.getString("enlargementMeta", ""))
2801:                     .putLong("pendingEnlargementAt", System.currentTimeMillis())
2802:                     .apply();
2803:             i = new Intent(this, SonoffArmService.class).setAction(SonoffArmService.ACTION_ARM_PRINT);
2804:             ensureExposureRecipeBase();
2805:             persistExposureRecipe();
2806:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_WIDTH, printWidthMs);
2807:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TIMING_METHOD, timingMethod);
2808:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_PRINT_SEQUENCE, printSequence == null ? "" : printSequence.encode());
2809:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_RECIPE_STATE, exposureRecipe.encode());
2810:         } else {
2811:             i = new Intent(this, SonoffArmService.class).setAction(SonoffArmService.ACTION_ARM_TEST);
2812:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_WIDTH, testWidthMs);
2813:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_COUNT, testCount);
2814:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_PAUSE, testPauseMs);
2815:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TIMING_METHOD, timingMethod);
2816:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_TARGETS, currentTestStripTargets());
2817:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_MASKING_METHOD, TimingMath.normalizeMaskingMethod(testStripMethod));
2818:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_FILTER_TYPE, ExposureRecipe.normalizeFilter(testBaseFilterType));
2819:             i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_FILTER_VALUE, ExposureRecipe.snap5(testBaseFilterValue));
2820:             if (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_HARD && splitSoftChosenMs > 0) {
2821:                 i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_PRE_EXPOSURE_MS, snap(splitSoftChosenMs, 500, 36_000_000));
2822:                 i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_PRE_EXPOSURE_FILTER_TYPE, ExposureRecipe.FILTER_YELLOW);
2823:                 i.putExtra(SonoffArmService.EXTRA_TEST_PRE_EXPOSURE_FILTER_VALUE, ExposureRecipe.snap5(splitSoftYellow));
2824:             }
2825:         }
2826:         startServiceCompat(i);
2827:     }

2829:     private void cancelCurrentCycle() {
2830:         if (!armed) return;
2831:         showAppConfirmDialog("ANNULLARE IL CICLO?",
2832:                 "L’uscita verrà spenta immediatamente e l’Inching verrà disattivato.",
2833:                 "ANNULLA CICLO", () -> {
2834:                     setStatusPresentation("ANNULLAMENTO", "Spengo l’uscita e disattivo Inching…", RED);
2835:                     cancelCycleButton.setEnabled(false);
2836:                     Intent i = new Intent(this, SonoffArmService.class).setAction(SonoffArmService.ACTION_CANCEL);
2837:                     startServiceCompat(i);
2838:                 }, "CONTINUA");
2839:     }

2841:     private boolean hasPendingTestStripChoice() {
2842:         SharedPreferences session = getSharedPreferences("log_session", MODE_PRIVATE);
2843:         long testAt = session.getLong("lastTestAt", 0L);
2844:         if (testAt <= 0L) return false;
2845:         long chosenAt = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).getLong("lastTestChooserShownAt", 0L);
2846:         return chosenAt < testAt;
2847:     }
2848: 
2849:     private void refreshPendingTestStripChoiceUi() {
2850:         if (testPendingChoiceButton == null) return;
2851:         boolean pending = hasPendingTestStripChoice();
2852:         testPendingChoiceButton.setVisibility(pending ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
2853:         if (pending) testPendingChoiceButton.setText(provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_SOFT ? "SCEGLI IL TEMPO MORBIDO" : (provinoFlow == PROVINO_SPLIT_HARD ? "SCEGLI IL TEMPO DURO" : "SCEGLI STRISCIA DEL PROVINO"));
2854:         testPendingChoiceButton.setEnabled(pending && !armed);
2855:         testPendingChoiceButton.setAlpha(testPendingChoiceButton.isEnabled() ? 1f : (darkroomMode ? 0.62f : 0.45f));
2856:     }

2871:         long testAt = session.getLong("lastTestAt", 0L);

3035:         printSequence=next;

3052:     private void disarm() {
3053:         if (device == null || !device.isValid()) {
3054:             stateText.setText("Nessun SONOFF DIY verificato");
3055:             return;
3056:         }
3057:         stateText.setTextColor(MUTED);
3058:         stateText.setText("Ripristino ON/OFF normale…");
3059:         Intent i = new Intent(this, SonoffArmService.class).setAction(SonoffArmService.ACTION_DISARM);
3060:         startServiceCompat(i);
3061:     }
3062: 
3063:     private void startServiceCompat(Intent intent) {
3064:         if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) {
3065:             try {
3066:                 java.lang.reflect.Method m = Context.class.getMethod("startForegroundService", Intent.class);
3067:                 m.invoke(this, intent);
3068:                 return;
3069:             } catch (Exception ignored) {}
3070:         }
3071:         startService(intent);
3072:     }

3074:     private void adjustPrintTime(int direction) {
3075:         if (armed) return;
3076:         if (TimingMath.isFStop(timingMethod)) setPrintTime(TimingMath.quarterStop(printWidthMs, direction, 500, 36_000_000));
3077:         else setPrintTime(printWidthMs + direction * 500);
3078:     }
3079: 
3080:     private void adjustTestTime(int direction) {
3081:         if (armed) return;
3082:         if (TimingMath.isFStop(timingMethod)) setTestTime(TimingMath.quarterStop(testWidthMs, direction, 500, 30_000));
3083:         else setTestTime(testWidthMs + direction * 500);
3084:     }
3085: 
3086:     private void setPrintTime(int ms) {
3087:         if (armed) return;
3088:         printWidthMs = snap(ms, 500, 36_000_000);
3089:         if (exposureRecipe == null) exposureRecipe = new ExposureRecipe();
3090:         if (exposureRecipe.hasBase()) exposureRecipe.operationalBaseMs = printWidthMs;
3091:         SharedPreferences.Editor edit = getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putInt("printWidthMs", printWidthMs);
3092:         if (exposureRecipe.hasBase()) edit.putString("exposureRecipe", exposureRecipe.encode());
3093:         edit.apply();
3094:         printTimeText.setText(formatTime(printWidthMs));
3095:         updatePrintSequenceUi();
3096:         applyModeUi();
3097:     }
3098: 
3099:     private void setTestTime(int ms) {
3100:         if (armed) return;
3101:         testWidthMs = snap(ms, 500, 30_000);
3102:         getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putInt("testWidthMs", testWidthMs).apply();
3103:         testTimeText.setText(formatTime(testWidthMs));
3104:         updateCumulativeTimes();
3105:         applyModeUi();
3106:     }
3107: 
3108:     private void setTestCount(int n) {
3109:         if (armed) return;
3110:         testCount = Math.max(2, Math.min(20, n));
3111:         getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putInt("testCount", testCount).apply();
3112:         testCountText.setText(String.valueOf(testCount));
3113:         updateCumulativeTimes();
3114:         applyModeUi();
3115:     }
3116: 
3117:     private void setTestPause(int ms) {

3119:         testPauseMs = snap(ms, 500, 60_000);
3120:         getSharedPreferences("ui", MODE_PRIVATE).edit().putInt("testPauseMs", testPauseMs).apply();
3121:         testPauseText.setText(formatTime(testPauseMs));
